# Interactive per-rally selection → reel builder (RallyPicker)

> **Status:** `DRAFT` — owner-gated (scope: preview endpoint, multi-video entry; and any `/api/compile` default change) · **Owner:** Avi · **Created:** 2026-06-20 · **Last updated:** 2026-06-20
> **Lifecycle:** `DRAFT → IN PROGRESS → DONE → archived` (move to `docs/archives/` when DONE)
> **Tracking anchors:** §7 progress tracker is the source of truth; indexed in `docs/README.md`; pointer in `NEXT_STEPS.md` + memory `current-state` once work starts.
> **Relation:** second major tracked work item in the `khelsutra` repo (peer of `REQUEST_ACCESS_FORM.md`); mirrors the engine repo's `PROJECT_DOC_TEMPLATE` discipline. Primarily realizes alpha screen **A5 Highlights** (engine repo `UI_PROPOSAL.md:47`, exec-plan **M4** `UI_ALPHA_EXECUTION_PLAN.md:39`); its correction-loop extension realizes **A4 Rally Review** (`UI_PROPOSAL.md:46`, exec-plan **M3** `:38`). Consumes the engine's `/api/query` + `/api/compile` contract.
> **Honesty note:** every engine-fact claim is tagged `[verified]` (read against engine code this session), `[design]` (proposed here), or `[researched]` (needs sign-off). Not legal/financial advice.

---

## 0. TL;DR
Build the **pick-rallies → reel** surface as a **hybrid**: a selectable rally **list/grid** is the workhorse; a **deterministic query bar** ("rallies over 10s, longest first") pre-selects into the **same** selection set; a sticky footer compiles the selection into one downloadable MP4. The entire MVP runs on **existing engine endpoints** — `POST /api/query` → `POST /api/compile` → `GET /api/highlights` `[verified backend/main.py:331-340, 342-396, 307-328]` — with **zero new engine intelligence and zero new endpoints**. The single most important caveat: queries and chips are scoped to **length / count / order / shot-count only**; shot-type, player, and score are **roadmap-only** (no detector, no column) and must not be faked. The biggest UX-vs-effort tension: **no per-rally preview is possible today** — only the *final compiled reel* can stream — so MVP selects on text metadata, and visual preview/timeline is a deliberate later milestone.

## 1. Problem & goal
Today the alpha can detect rallies and stitch a reel, but reel-building is either a **silent `stitching.min_shot_count` threshold** `[verified backend/main.py:362-383]` or a static checkbox list whose data plumbing is even broken — the bundled static UI reads `data.rallies` while the API returns `play_segments`, so it renders nothing `[verified backend/api/static/app.js:344 vs backend/main.py:340]`. The owner's framing is **dynamic per-video selection, not a fixed threshold**: a coach should say "give me the long rallies from this session" and get exactly those, then fine-tune by hand.

**The concrete outcome ("good"):** A coach uploads a 40-minute session; the engine finds 60 rallies. They type *"top 15 longest"*, eyeball the auto-selected cards, untick two warm-up rallies, name it *"Tuesday — long points"*, and download a ~6-minute reel — without learning any threshold knob.

**Goal:** the smallest *honest* surface that turns engine-detected rallies into a user-curated, downloadable reel, with both **fast** (query) and **precise** (checkbox) selection sharing **one** state.

**Non-goals (alpha):** no conversational LLM (`UI_PROPOSAL.md:66` defers it); no shot-type/player/score filters (no data exists); no per-rally video preview while it requires unbuilt streaming; no multi-tenant auth (single-box alpha).

## 2. Decisions locked
| # | Decision | Source / date | Implication |
|---|----------|---------------|-------------|
| D1 | Dominant surface = selectable rally **list/grid** | Synthesis 2026-06-20 (Grid scored 15, fit-to-data 4) | Scales to long matches via bulk+filter; shows honest fields only |
| D2 | Fast entry = **deterministic** client-side `parseQuery()` (no LLM) | `[verified UI_PROPOSAL.md:66]` conversational QA deferred | Predictable, unit-testable, offline, zero API cost |
| D3 | **One** shared `Set<segment_id>`, mutated by query + checkbox + (future) timeline | Synthesis 2026-06-20 | No second compile path can diverge from what the user sees |
| D4 | Compile via existing `POST /api/compile {video_id, segment_ids, output_filename}` | `[verified backend/main.py:342-396]` | Selection order is harmless — stitcher `_merge_overlapping` de-dupes `[verified compiler.py:80-105, 311-317]` |
| D5 | Query grammar (alpha) = length, count, order, **shot_count only** | `[verified database.py:122-135, gemini.py:470-485]` | Only fields the payload actually returns; grey out the rest |
| D6 | MVP backend = **zero new endpoints** | `[verified]` reuse map (§4) | Ships the full loop on the already-shipped contract |
| D7 | Replace the silent `min_shot_count` cut with **explicit user selection** | Owner framing + `[verified main.py:362-383]` | UI *warns* about the floor; it never hides why a rally vanished |

## 3. Foundation — what the engine gives us today  `[verified this session]`
The whole design rests on the engine's real, already-shipped contract. Read against the engine repo (`badminton-highlight-indexer`):

- **The rally store is `play_segments`** — columns `id, video_id, start_time, end_time, duration, server, receiver, metadata` `[verified database.py:124-135]`. `id` is the integer the UI selects and passes to compile; `start_time/end_time/duration` are REAL seconds (duration computed at insert).
- **Listing rallies = `POST /api/query {video_id}` → `{play_segments:[...]}`** — the *only* rally-list path today; it JSON-parses `metadata` and joins `events` `[verified main.py:331-340, database.py:427-475]`.
- **Building a reel = `POST /api/compile {video_id, segment_ids:[int], output_filename}`** — loads `videos.filepath`, filters all segments to the requested `segment_ids`, applies the `min_shot_count` floor, extracts `time_pairs=[(start,end)]`, and calls `VideoCompiler.compile_highlights(raw_path, segments, name)` `[verified main.py:342-396, compiler.py:303]`, which **merges overlaps** then per-clip re-encodes → concat → one MP4 `[verified compiler.py:311-317]`. Selection order/duplicates are therefore safe.
- **Downloading = `GET /api/highlights/{video_id}/{filename}`** — streams the compiled MP4. Compile returns a **server-local filepath, NOT a URL** `[verified main.py:394]`, so the SPA constructs this download URL itself. The compiled reel is the **only streamable video that exists today** `[verified main.py:307-328]`.
- **The floor knob is readable** — `GET /api/config` exposes `stitching.min_shot_count` `[verified main.py:121-123]`, so the UI can warn before a selected rally is silently dropped.
- **Job entry** — `GET /api/jobs/{job_id}` returns `result.video_id` `[verified main.py:274-293]`, which is how the MVP reaches a video without a (nonexistent) list-videos endpoint.

**Honest fields vs fabricated/roadmap (critical for the UI):**
- **Honest today** (default `default_segmenter: "gemini"`, per `config.json` `[verified]`): `start_time`, `end_time`, `duration`, and `metadata.shot_count` (Gemini's `shuttle_exchange_count`, fallback `max(3, int(dur/0.8))`) + `metadata.shot_count_confidence` (a **string**, often `"Unknown"`) `[verified gemini.py:470-485]`. On the gemini path `server`/`receiver` are written `None` ("no fabricated data") `[verified gemini.py:480-487]`.
- **Fabricated — do NOT surface:** the `motion` demo segmenter fills `server`/`receiver` via `random.sample(...)`, a random `shot_count`, and random `events` (serve/smash/foul/winner) `[verified motion.py:191-198]`. `query` returns these columns + `events`, but they are not ground truth and must stay hidden.
- **Roadmap — no column, no detector:** shot-type per shot, player identity, match/point score, game/set grouping. None exist anywhere in the schema; do not consume them `[verified database.py whole-schema 122-148]`.
- **Correction-loop columns exist but are dead:** `candidate_segments.human_label/notes/confirmed_segment_id` are defined but no endpoint writes them `[verified database.py:166-168]` — that's the M4 corpus extension (engine PR-4/B6, `UI_ALPHA_EXECUTION_PLAN.md`).

## 4. Design / architecture

**Reuse map (existing — `[verified]`, no change for MVP):** `POST /api/query` (list) → `POST /api/compile` (stitch) → `GET /api/highlights/{video_id}/{filename}` (download); `GET /api/config` (floor) ; `GET /api/jobs/{id}` (carry `result.video_id`). **All net-new code is the SPA** in `khelsutra` `web/` (Vite + React + TS + Tailwind, per `web/README.md`), plus a small set of *optional* real-gap engine enablers (§4.3) — none required for MVP.

**4.1 Components (net-new SPA)**
- **QueryBar + `parseQuery()`** — a *pure, deterministic, unit-tested* string → `{filter, sort}` compiler over `duration` / `ordinal` / `shot_count`. Renders a **parsed-pill** ("filter: duration > 10s · sort: duration desc") so the interpretation is transparent and hand-correctable; greys out shot-type/player/score tokens with an inline "not available yet". No LLM, no network call.
- **RallyList/Grid** — one card per `play_segment`: ordinal (by `start_time`), `mm:ss` start, duration (`end−start`), and a shot-count + confidence chip **only when `metadata.shot_count` is present** (absent on detector paths that don't write it). Checkbox per card; the thumbnail area is a placeholder (no endpoint yet).
- **`useRallySelection()` hook** — owns `selectedIds:Set<number>`, `displayOrder`, `apply(queryResult)`, `toggle(id)`, and bulk Select-all / Clear / Invert. Unit-tested (matches the exec-plan's "API client + review-state logic" bar).
- **SelectionFooter (sticky)** — live "N clips / total M:SS"; a **`min_shot_count` warning** when a selected rally would be dropped at compile (read from `GET /api/config`); reel-name input; one **Build** button.
- **CompileResult** — `<video src=GET /api/highlights/...>` preview of the *compiled reel* + a download link.
- **TypedApiClient** — `query`, `compile`, `buildDownloadUrl`; the typed seam to the engine.

**4.2 Data flow** `[verified anchors inline]`
Gemini segmenter (process time) writes `play_segments {id, start/end_time, duration, metadata.shot_count/confidence/detector}` `[gemini.py:461-498]`
→ SPA `POST /api/query {video_id}` → `{play_segments:[...]}` `[main.py:331-340]` (**fix** the static UI's `data.rallies` read → `play_segments` `[app.js:344]`)
→ normalize to `RallyCard[]` + one shared `selectedIds:Set`; `server`/`receiver`/`events` are present in the payload but motion-only fabrications → **not surfaced** `[motion.py:191-198]`
→ all input modes mutate the **same** `selectedIds`: (a) `parseQuery()` predicate over duration/ordinal/shot_count; (b) checkbox toggle + bulk ops; (c) *(post-MVP)* timeline band
→ footer recomputes count + Σduration **client-side**, and warns for any selected rally with `shot_count < stitching.min_shot_count` `[main.py:362-383]`
→ **Build** → `POST /api/compile {video_id, segment_ids: [...selectedIds], output_filename}` → stitcher de-dupes via `_merge_overlapping` `[compiler.py:311-317]`, cuts `time_pairs` from `videos.filepath`, re-encodes per clip → concat → one MP4 `[main.py:386-393, compiler.py:303]`
→ compile returns a **server-local filepath, not a URL** `[main.py:394]` → SPA builds `GET /api/highlights/{video_id}/{output_filename}` to preview in `<video>` + download `[main.py:307-328]`.

**4.3 Engine API additions (none for MVP; tagged against real gaps)**
| Endpoint | Purpose | Status |
|---|---|---|
| `POST /api/query` | List rallies (only path) — fix SPA to read `play_segments` | **exists** |
| `POST /api/compile` | Stitch selected `segment_ids` → MP4 | **exists** |
| `GET /api/highlights/{video_id}/{filename}` | Stream/download compiled reel | **exists** |
| `GET /api/config` | Read `stitching.min_shot_count` for the warning | **exists** |
| `GET /api/jobs/{job_id}` | Carry `result.video_id` into the picker | **exists** |
| `POST /api/compile` → add `download_url` | Return `/api/highlights/...` so SPA stops hand-building it (additive, low-risk; today returns only a filepath `main.py:394`) | **modify** |
| `GET /api/health` | Engine-offline banner ping the SPA is specified to use (`HOSTING_PLAN.md:42`) | **new** |
| `GET /api/videos/{video_id}/rallies` | RESTful/cacheable alias returning the same `{play_segments}` shape | **new** |
| `GET /api/videos` | List a user's videos for a Match picker (only `get_video(id)` exists, `database.py:418`) — needs DB enumerate + identity scoping | **new** |
| `GET /api/videos/{video_id}/source` (Range) *or* `/clip?start=&end=` | Stream source/per-span video so a rally previews before selection — neither exists | **new** |
| `GET /api/videos/{video_id}/rallies/{segment_id}/thumbnail` | Per-rally poster still (no thumbnail column; WASB frames are transient) | **new** |
| `PATCH /api/segments/{id}` | Persist accept/reject/nudge → `candidate_segments.human_label/notes/...` (columns exist, unwritten — corpus loop) | **new** |

**⚠ Dead-ends / do-NOT:** never surface `server`/`receiver`/`events` (motion-only randoms `[motion.py:191-198]`); never consume shot-type/player/score (no data); never render a confidence **meter** — `shot_count_confidence` is a Gemini *string* (often `"Unknown"`) and `play_segments` has **no** numeric confidence column.

## 5. Risk table
| Risk | Severity | Mitigation |
|---|---|---|
| **No source/clip/frame/thumbnail endpoint** — only compiled reels stream `[main.py:307-328]` | High (UX) | MVP selects on text metadata + previews the *final* reel; preview/timeline deferred to M3 behind a new endpoint |
| `min_shot_count` silently drops selected rallies `[main.py:362-383]` | High (looks broken) | Footer warns *before* Build; D7 makes selection explicit; an override is an owner gate (Launch-Report-worthy `[memory: launch-report-convention]`) |
| No match-duration field — a timeline must derive `total = max(end_time)`, mis-rendering pre/post dead air | Med | Timeline deferred to M3; never over-promise it as a true scrubber until source streams |
| `shot_count` is an *estimate* (`shuttle_exchange_count` w/ `max(3,int(dur/0.8))` fallback) `[gemini.py:470-485]` | Med | Label "approx."; confidence shown as the raw string, not a meter |
| No auth / user scoping anywhere (CORS `*`, `videos.user_id` never set/filtered) | High for multi-tester | Fine for single-box local alpha; a ship-blocker for any shared host — gate `GET /api/videos` behind PR-3 scoping |
| compile returns a filepath not a URL `[main.py:394]` | Low | Client builds the URL for MVP; `download_url` field (M1) removes the brittleness |
| Surface sprawl (query + grid + future timeline + future correction) | Med | Deliberate IA: query+grid = one screen; correction = separate ⭐ corpus screen (owner call, §8) |

## 6. Honest limits — what this does NOT do
A green SPA build + passing `parseQuery`/selection unit tests prove the **picker logic only** — **not** that the engine's rally boundaries are correct (the measured **rally-END** weakness is a separate quality track, unaffected by this UI). "Conversational" here means a **deterministic four-dimension parser**, not natural-language understanding. Nothing is implemented in `web/` yet — this is `[design]`. The feature curates *what the engine already found*; it cannot surface a rally the detector missed, nor split/merge a mis-bounded one (that's the M4 correction loop).

## 7. Deliverables & progress tracker  ⟵ **source of truth**
Legend: ☐ Todo · ◐ In progress · ☑ Done · ⛔ Blocked/gated. **One small PR per row**, branched from `origin/master`, no stacking. CI (`npm test`) must stay green; merge to `master` auto-deploys the site (docs/`web/` changes don't touch the live `site/` Worker).

| ID | Deliverable | Depends on | Gated? | Status | PR |
|----|-------------|------------|--------|--------|----|
| P0 | This design doc + `docs/README.md` + `NEXT_STEPS.md` pointers | — | No | ◐ | (this PR) |
| P1 | SPA scaffold (Vite+React+TS+Tailwind) in `web/` + typed API client; dev proxy to engine `:8000` | P0 | No | ☐ | — |
| P2 | RallyList/Grid (honest fields only) + `useRallySelection()` hook (unit-tested) | P1 | No | ☐ | — |
| P3 | `parseQuery()` + parsed-pill + example chips (unit-tested; greys out unavailable tokens) | P2 | No | ☐ | — |
| P4 | SelectionFooter + `min_shot_count` warning (from `GET /api/config`) | P2 | No | ☐ | — |
| P5 | Build → `POST /api/compile` → client-built download URL + `<video>` preview of the reel | P2 | No | ☐ | — |
| P6 | *(engine repo)* `GET /api/health` + offline banner; `/api/compile` returns `download_url` | — | No | ☐ | — |
| P7 | *(engine repo)* `GET /api/videos/{id}/rallies` RESTful alias | — | No | ☐ | — |
| P8 | *(engine repo)* `GET /api/videos` + PR-3 identity scoping → A1 Matches screen | P7 | ⛔ owner | ☐ | — |
| P9 | *(engine repo)* source/clip stream + timeline + per-rally preview/thumbnail | P8 | ⛔ owner | ☐ | — |
| P10 | *(engine repo)* `PATCH /api/segments/{id}` corpus loop (PR-4/B6) → A4 Rally Review | P9 | ⛔ owner | ☐ | — |

**Milestone rollup:** **M0/MVP = P1–P5** (pick→reel on existing API, zero new engine endpoints) · **M1 = P6–P7** (RESTful + reliability polish) · **M2 = P8** (multi-video home) · **M3 = P9** (per-rally preview/timeline) · **M4 = P10** (corpus correction loop) · **M5** = swap `parseQuery()` for localized NL behind the same seam (gated on shot/player data landing).

## 8. Open questions — owner / external
**Blocking gates (need a decision before the gated rows):**
1. **Default selection state** — load rallies all-ON (curate-by-removal; matches today's "all rallies" reel) or all-OFF (curate-by-addition)? *Owner product call.*
2. **Is preview-before-select a ship-blocker?** Text-metadata selection is acceptable for MVP, but is the "feel" the owner wants blocked until a source/clip-stream endpoint exists? This is the single biggest backend lift. *Owner scope call.*
3. **MVP entry** — ship job-tethered (carry `result.video_id`, no `GET /api/videos`) vs pull multi-video + PR-3 scoping into MVP? *Owner scope call.*
4. **`min_shot_count` on the manual path** — keep the server-side floor silently dropping user-selected rallies (warn in UI) or let an explicit checkbox override it? Touches `/api/compile` default behavior → **Launch Report** convention applies. *Owner product call.*

**Nice-to-knows:**
5. May user-facing copy call `parseQuery()` "conversational", given conversational QA is explicitly deferred (`UI_PROPOSAL.md:66`)? Risk of over-promising NL. *Owner positioning/honesty call.*
6. One screen for query+grid (recommended) vs a separate ⭐ corpus/correction screen for A4? *Owner positioning call.*

## 9. Definition of done
- [ ] From a finished job, a user can list rallies, query/select, and download a reel — on **existing** endpoints.
- [ ] Only honest fields (ordinal / duration / shot_count+confidence-when-present) are shown; fabricated `server`/`receiver`/`events` are hidden.
- [ ] `parseQuery()` covers length/count/order/shot_count, greys out unavailable tokens, shows a visible parsed-pill, and is unit-tested.
- [ ] Selection hook is unit-tested; the `min_shot_count` floor is surfaced, not hidden.
- [ ] `npm run build` (and `npm test`) clean; the static UI's `data.rallies` bug is gone (or the static UI is superseded by the SPA).
- [ ] §7 tracker + this DoD updated; doc archived when P1–P5 land (MVP).

## 10. References
**Engine code anchors `[verified this session]`:** `backend/main.py:121-123` (config), `:274-293` (jobs), `:307-328` (highlights download), `:331-340` (query), `:342-396` (compile + `min_shot_count` floor + filepath-not-URL); `backend/infrastructure/database.py:124-135` (play_segments), `:166-168` (dead correction columns), `:418` (no list-videos), `:427-475` (query read path + events join); `backend/pipeline/segmenters/gemini.py:461-498, 470-485` (honest shot_count, server/receiver=None); `backend/pipeline/segmenters/motion.py:191-198` (fabricated fields); `backend/pipeline/stitcher/compiler.py:80-105, 303, 311-317` (merge/de-dupe + entrypoint); `backend/api/static/app.js:344` (`data.rallies` bug); `config.json` (`default_segmenter: "gemini"`).

khelsutra was already at remote head (`master` = `origin/master` = #10, clean tree); the fetch just pruned merged PR branches. I cut my work branch **explicitly from `origin/master`**, staged only my 3 doc paths, and verified only my commit is ahead before pushing — safe against the concurrent droplet/GPU sessions sharing this checkout.

## ✅ Tracked design doc shipped → [khelsutra#11](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/11)
`DRAFT`, **owner-gated** (not auto-merged — it embeds product decisions). CI green (`site` npm test 20/20, Workers Builds pass). Files: `docs/PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md` + index/NEXT_STEPS pointers.

**How it was built:** a multi-agent Workflow (map the real engine contract → 3 independent designs: timeline / grid / conversational → judge + synthesize), then **I personally re-read every load-bearing claim against engine source** before writing a word — the `play_segments` schema, the `app.js` `data.rallies` bug, `_merge_overlapping` de-dupe, the `min_shot_count` floor, the `gemini` shot_count fallback, the `motion` fabrications, and `default_segmenter: gemini`. Nothing in the doc is asserted that I didn't verify.

**The headline finding:** the **MVP needs zero new engine endpoints** — it rides the contract already shipped:
```
POST /api/query → user picks segment_ids → POST /api/compile → GET /api/highlights
```
Recommendation: a **hybrid** — selectable rally **grid** (workhorse) + a **deterministic `parseQuery()`** bar (no LLM; length/count/order/shot_count only) writing into **one shared selection set** + a sticky compile footer. Honest scope baked in: shot-type/player/score are roadmap (no column, no detector); `server`/`receiver`/`events` are `motion`-only fabrications and stay hidden; **per-rally preview is the one real blocker** (only the *compiled* reel can stream today) so it's a deliberate later milestone.

## Your call — 4 decisions gate the build (doc §8)
| # | Decision | My recommended default |
|---|----------|------------------------|
| 1 | Default selection **ON** (curate-by-removal) or **OFF**? | **ON** — matches today's "all rallies" reel; user removes warm-ups |
| 2 | Is **preview-before-select** a ship-blocker for alpha? | **No** — ship text-metadata MVP; add the source/clip stream endpoint next |
| 3 | MVP **job-tethered** (carry `video_id`) or pull in multi-video + auth? | **Job-tethered** — smallest honest slice; multi-video needs identity scoping |
| 4 | Does explicit selection **override** the silent `min_shot_count` floor? | **Warn, don't override** for MVP (override touches a compile default → Launch Report) |

If those defaults look right, I can proceed to **P1 (SPA scaffold + typed API client in `web/`)** and build the MVP (P1–P5, all on existing endpoints). Want me to start with those defaults, or hold for your review of the doc first?

### 77. user (2026-06-20T08:44:35.692Z)

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	# Asserts the two live hosts (Cloudflare + droplet) serve BYTE-IDENTICAL content.
3	# Catches deploy drift the unit/integration tests can't: they verify the code works on
4	# each host, NOT that the two live URLs are in sync. (.gitattributes keeps line endings
5	# identical so a stale scp can't masquerade as a "difference".)
6	#
7	#   CF_URL=https://khelsutra.guru VM_URL=http://168.144.126.176:8787 scripts/parity-check.sh
8	set -euo pipefail
9	CF="${CF_URL:-https://khelsutra.guru}"
10	VM="${VM_URL:-http://168.144.126.176:8787}"
11	UA="Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; khelsutra-parity)"
12	fail=0
13	for p in "" capture; do
14	  a=$(curl -fsS -A "$UA" --max-time 25 "$CF/$p?x=$RANDOM" | md5sum | cut -d' ' -f1)
15	  b=$(curl -fsS -A "$UA" --max-time 25 "$VM/$p?x=$RANDOM" | md5sum | cut -d' ' -f1)
16	  if [ "$a" = "$b" ]; then echo "OK    /$p  $a"; else echo "DRIFT /$p  cf=$a  vm=$b"; fail=1; fi
17	done
18	if [ "$fail" = 0 ]; then echo "PARITY: in sync"; else
19	  echo "PARITY: DRIFT — re-sync the droplet from git (LF):"
20	  echo "  git -C <repo> archive HEAD:site | ssh root@<vm> tar -x -C /srv/khelsutra-site/site"
21	  exit 1
22	fi
23

### 79. user (2026-06-20T08:45:44.053Z)

1	// Storage adapters for Request-Access submissions.
2	//
3	// PORTABILITY (see docs/PORTABILITY.md): the business logic (validate / toRow /
4	// handleRequest in index.js) is runtime-agnostic — ALL database coupling lives here.
5	// The Cloudflare Worker uses d1Store(env.DB); an always-on VM uses sqliteStore(db)
6	// over node:sqlite (or better-sqlite3). Same schema.sql, same INSERT — zero dialect
7	// change. To move hosts, swap the adapter, not the logic.
8	
9	export const INSERT_SQL =
10	  `INSERT INTO access_requests
11	     (segment, name, email, city, org_name, courts, has_gpu, storage, message, consent, country, user_agent)
12	   VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`;
13	
14	export const insertArgs = (r) => [
15	  r.segment, r.name, r.email, r.city, r.org_name, r.courts,
16	  r.has_gpu, r.storage, r.message, r.consent, r.country, r.user_agent,
17	];
18	
19	// Cloudflare D1 (async): prepare(sql).bind(...args).run()
20	export function d1Store(DB) {
21	  return {
22	    async insertAccessRequest(r) {
23	      await DB.prepare(INSERT_SQL).bind(...insertArgs(r)).run();
24	    },
25	  };
26	}
27	
28	// node:sqlite / better-sqlite3 (sync): prepare(sql).run(...args). For the VM host.
29	export function sqliteStore(db) {
30	  const stmt = db.prepare(INSERT_SQL);
31	  return {
32	    async insertAccessRequest(r) {
33	      stmt.run(...insertArgs(r));
34	    },
35	  };
36	}

14	function freePort() {
15	  return new Promise((res) => {
16	    const s = createServer();
17	    s.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", () => { const p = s.address().port; s.close(() => res(p)); });
18	  });
19	}
20	async function waitUp(url, ms = 10000) {
21	  const end = Date.now() + ms;
22	  while (Date.now() < end) {
23	    try { await fetch(url); return; } catch { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 150)); }
24	  }
25	  throw new Error("server.js did not start listening in time");
26	}
27	
28	let proc, base, dbPath, dir;
29	
30	before(async () => {

32	  dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "ks-vm-"));

35	  const serverPath = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "server.js");
36	  proc = spawn(process.execPath, [serverPath], {
37	    env: { ...process.env, PORT: String(port), DB_PATH: dbPath },
38	    stdio: "ignore",
39	  });
40	  await waitUp(`${base}/`);
41	});
42	
43	after(() => {
44	  proc?.kill();
45	  try { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); } catch {}
46	});
47	
48	test("[VM host] server.js serves the form", async () => {
49	  const html = await (await fetch(`${base}/`)).text();
50	  assert.match(html, /id="af"/);
51	  assert.match(html, /Get into the KhelSutra alpha/);
52	});

54	test("[VM host] serves /capture and /og.png", async () => {
55	  assert.equal((await fetch(`${base}/capture`)).status, 200);
56	  assert.equal((await fetch(`${base}/og.png`)).status, 200);

63	    body: JSON.stringify({ segment: "academy", email: "harness@khelsutra.guru", has_gpu: "yes", storage: ["gdrive", "bucket"], consent: true }),

### 82. user (2026-06-20T08:45:58.616Z)

1	// Always-on VM host for the KhelSutra site — dependency-free (Node 22+ only).
2	// Serves public/ and POST /api/request-access into SQLite (node:sqlite), using the
3	// SAME handler logic as the Cloudflare Worker (src/index.js). This is the portability
4	// fallback "if Cloudflare acts out" — see docs/PORTABILITY.md.
5	//
6	//   PORT=8080 DB_PATH=/var/lib/khelsutra/access.db node server.js
7	//
8	import { createServer } from "node:http";
9	import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
10	import { join, extname, normalize } from "node:path";
11	import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";
12	import { handleRequest } from "./src/index.js";
13	import { sqliteStore } from "./src/store.js";
14	import { noopNotify } from "./src/notify.js";
15	
16	const PORT = Number(process.env.PORT) || 8080;
17	const ROOT = import.meta.dirname;
18	const PUBLIC = join(ROOT, "public");
19	const DB_PATH = process.env.DB_PATH || join(ROOT, "access.db");
20	
21	const db = new DatabaseSync(DB_PATH);
22	db.exec(await readFile(join(ROOT, "schema.sql"), "utf8")); // CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS — idempotent
23	const store = sqliteStore(db);
24	const notify = noopNotify(); // VM host: no email yet (add SMTP later — see docs/PORTABILITY.md)
25	
26	const TYPES = {
27	  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8", ".png": "image/png", ".svg": "image/svg+xml",
28	  ".css": "text/css", ".js": "text/javascript", ".ico": "image/x-icon", ".txt": "text/plain",
29	};
30	
31	async function toRequest(req) {
32	  const url = `http://${req.headers.host || "localhost"}${req.url}`;
33	  let body;
34	  if (req.method !== "GET" && req.method !== "HEAD") {
35	    const chunks = [];
36	    for await (const c of req) chunks.push(c);
37	    body = Buffer.concat(chunks);
38	  }
39	  return new Request(url, { method: req.method, headers: req.headers, body });
40	}
41	
42	async function send(res, response) {
43	  res.statusCode = response.status;
44	  for (const [k, v] of response.headers) res.setHeader(k, v);
45	  res.end(Buffer.from(await response.arrayBuffer()));
46	}
47	
48	async function serveStatic(pathname, res) {
49	  const rel = pathname === "/" ? "index.html"
50	    : pathname === "/capture" ? "capture.html"
51	    : pathname.replace(/^\/+/, "");
52	  const file = normalize(join(PUBLIC, rel));
53	  if (!file.startsWith(PUBLIC)) { res.statusCode = 403; return res.end("forbidden"); }
54	  try {
55	    const data = await readFile(file);
56	    res.setHeader("content-type", TYPES[extname(file)] || "application/octet-stream");
57	    res.end(data);
58	  } catch {
59	    res.statusCode = 404;
60	    res.end("not found");
61	  }
62	}
63	
64	createServer(async (req, res) => {
65	  try {
66	    const pathname = decodeURIComponent(new URL(`http://x${req.url}`).pathname);
67	    if (pathname === "/api/request-access") {
68	      if (req.method !== "POST") { res.statusCode = 405; return res.end('{"ok":false,"error":"method not allowed"}'); }
69	      return await send(res, await handleRequest(await toRequest(req), store, notify));
70	    }
71	    return await serveStatic(pathname, res);
72	  } catch (err) {
73	    res.statusCode = 500;
74	    res.end('{"ok":false,"error":"server error"}');
75	    console.error(err);
76	  }
77	}).listen(PORT, () => console.log(`khelsutra site listening on :${PORT}  (sqlite: ${DB_PATH})`));
78

7	  name       TEXT,

13	  storage    TEXT,            -- csv of gdrive,onedrive,dropbox,bucket,local,unsure
14	  message    TEXT,            -- free text
15	  consent    INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
16	  country    TEXT,            -- coarse, from cf-ipcountry (no raw IP stored)

### 101. assistant (2026-06-20T08:50:08.839Z)

// Deploy-config guard — the live Cloudflare deploy is git-connected (merge to `master`
// auto-deploys via Workers Builds). A silent drift in wrangler.toml would break the LIVE
// site with NO test noticing: PR #5 was exactly this — the wrangler `name` didn't match
// the connected Worker, so builds missed khelsutra.guru. These assertions pin the deploy
// invariants. String-based on purpose (the site is dependency-free; no TOML parser dep).
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFileSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";

const ROOT = join(import.meta.dirname, "..");
const toml = readFileSync(join(ROOT, "wrangler.toml"), "utf8");
const has = (re, msg) => assert.match(toml, re, msg);

test("wrangler `name` matches the git-connected Worker that owns khelsutra.guru", () => {
  // If this drifts, Workers Builds deploys a DIFFERENT worker and the live site goes stale (PR #5).
  has(/^\s*name\s*=\s*"hidden-king-khelsutra"/m, "wrangler name must stay 'hidden-king-khelsutra'");
});

test("wrangler `main` and the assets directory point at paths that exist", () => {
  const main = toml.match(/^\s*main\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"/m)?.[1];
  assert.ok(main, "main = ... is missing from wrangler.toml");
  assert.ok(existsSync(join(ROOT, main)), `main entry '${main}' does not exist`);

  const dir = toml.match(/\[assets\][\s\S]*?directory\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"/)?.[1];
  assert.ok(dir, "[assets] directory is missing from wrangler.toml");
  assert.ok(existsSync(join(ROOT, dir)), `assets directory '${dir}' does not exist`);
});

test("D1 binding is wired (the Request-Access form persists to D1)", () => {
  has(/\[\[d1_databases\]\]/, "missing [[d1_databases]]");
  has(/binding\s*=\s*"DB"/, "D1 binding must be 'DB' (code uses env.DB)");
  has(/database_name\s*=\s*"khelsutra-access"/, "D1 database_name drift");
  has(/database_id\s*=\s*"[0-9a-f-]{36}"/, "D1 database_id missing/!uuid — deploy binds nothing");
});

test("email-on-registration send binding + notify vars are present", () => {
  // The Email Routing send binding is what emails the owner on each registration (PR #8).
  has(/\[\[send_email\]\]/, "missing [[send_email]] binding");
  has(/name\s*=\s*"NOTIFY"/, "send_email binding must be named 'NOTIFY' (code uses env.NOTIFY)");
  has(/NOTIFY_FROM\s*=\s*"/, "NOTIFY_FROM var missing");
  has(/NOTIFY_TO\s*=\s*"/, "NOTIFY_TO var missing");
});

### 104. assistant (2026-06-20T08:50:22.875Z)

// Static-site integrity — the landing page + capture checklist + OG image ARE the public
// product. server.test.js proves the VM host *serves* "/", "/capture", "/og.png"; this
// guards the CONTENT contract so a broken edit (deleted asset, broken form wiring, dead
// capture link, missing/!absolute OG image) fails CI instead of shipping to the live site.
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFileSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";

const PUBLIC = join(import.meta.dirname, "..", "public");
const read = (f) => readFileSync(join(PUBLIC, f), "utf8");
const size = (f) => statSync(join(PUBLIC, f)).size;

test("core public assets exist and are non-empty", () => {
  for (const f of ["index.html", "capture.html", "og.png"]) {
    assert.ok(size(f) > 0, `public/${f} is missing or empty`);
  }
});

test("landing page wires the Request-Access form to the API", () => {
  const html = read("index.html");
  assert.match(html, /id="af"/, "the #af form is gone");
  assert.match(html, /\/api\/request-access/, "form no longer posts to /api/request-access");
  assert.match(html, /name="company"[^>]*tabindex="-1"/, "honeypot field removed (bots will get through)");
  for (const field of ["segment", "has_gpu", "storage"]) {
    assert.match(html, new RegExp(`name="${field}"`), `form lost the '${field}' field (a demand signal)`);
  }
});

test("OG + canonical meta point at the live https origin", () => {
  const html = read("index.html");
  assert.match(html, /property="og:image"\s+content="https:\/\/khelsutra\.guru\/og\.png"/, "og:image must be the absolute live URL");
  assert.match(html, /property="og:url"\s+content="https:\/\/khelsutra\.guru"/, "og:url drift");
});

test("the capture checklist is linked and is a real page", () => {
  assert.match(read("index.html"), /href="\/capture(\.html)?"/, "footer capture-checklist link is gone");
  const cap = read("capture.html");
  assert.match(cap, /<html/i, "capture.html is not HTML");
  assert.match(cap, /capture checklist/i, "capture.html lost its checklist content");
});

### 106. assistant (2026-06-20T08:50:31.160Z)

# Live-host parity — the site runs on TWO hosts (Cloudflare Worker + the DO droplet's
# Node/SQLite twin) that must serve byte-identical pages. The unit suite proves the code
# works on each host; it CANNOT see that the two LIVE URLs have drifted (e.g. a merge
# auto-deployed Cloudflare but the droplet wasn't re-synced from git). This runs the
# existing scripts/parity-check.sh against both live URLs on a schedule — NOT on PRs, so
# it never blocks development; a failure surfaces real production drift to the owner.
name: live-parity

on:
  schedule:
    - cron: "17 6 * * *" # daily ~06:17 UTC
  workflow_dispatch: {} # and on demand

# Avoid overlapping runs if a manual dispatch races the cron.
concurrency:
  group: live-parity
  cancel-in-progress: false

jobs:
  parity:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - name: Compare the two live hosts byte-for-byte
        env:
          CF_URL: https://khelsutra.guru
          VM_URL: http://168.144.126.176:8787
        run: bash site/scripts/parity-check.sh

Both PRs are merged and the new CI is verified working end-to-end. Summary:

## ✅ Merged (both green, squashed, branches deleted)
- **[khelsutra#11](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/11)** — the RallyPicker design doc. Before merging I locked your approved decisions into it: **D8** default selection ON · **D9** preview not a ship-blocker · **D10** job-tethered MVP · **D11** warn-don't-override the floor. Status flipped `DRAFT → IN PROGRESS`.
- **[khelsutra#12](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/12)** — the new regression CI.

master is at `eeb8a48`, tree clean, no open PRs.

## ✅ New CI to catch regressions
The site auto-deploys on merge, so the guards target what could silently ship broken to the live site — all dependency-free:

| Guard | Catches |
|---|---|
| `site/src/deploy-config.test.js` | `wrangler.toml` drift — Worker `name` (**the #5 incident**: wrong-worker deploy → stale live site), `main`/assets paths, D1 binding, the Email-Routing send binding + vars |
| `site/src/assets.test.js` | broken static site — missing `index/capture/og` assets, broken form wiring (action/honeypot/fields), wrong OG/canonical URLs, dead capture link |
| `.github/workflows/parity.yml` | **Cloudflare ↔ droplet drift** (the two-host sync) — runs `parity-check.sh` daily + on-demand, **non-blocking** (schedule/dispatch only, never blocks a PR) |
| `npm test` → `node --test` discovery | a new `*.test.js` being silently left out of CI |

Suite went **20 → 28 green**. I dispatched the live-parity workflow once to prove it works: it ran green and reported `OK /`, `OK /capture`, **`PARITY: in sync`** — so the two live hosts are currently byte-identical too.

## Next: build the MVP (P1–P5)
With the design approved and decisions locked, the next deliverable is **P1 — the SPA scaffold in `web/`** (Vite + React + TS + Tailwind + a typed API client, dev-proxied to the engine at `:8000`), then P2–P5 build the grid + `parseQuery()` + selection footer + compile→download, all on the existing engine endpoints (zero new backend).

Want me to start on **P1** now? Each of P1–P5 will be its own small PR off `origin/master`, CI-green, and — since they're build work on the approved design (not new product/strategy) — I can merge them on green per our working agreement unless you'd rather review each first.

### 135. assistant (2026-06-20T08:56:30.508Z)

Good question — and it directly shapes both the build and this design, so let me answer it head-on before I go further.

**Yes — live CUJ replay is part of how I'll develop this, not an afterthought.** Concretely:

- **UI CUJs via the preview/browser tools** — I run the SPA dev server and drive the journey headlessly (snapshot → fill the query bar "top 15 longest" → assert auto-selection → toggle a card → assert footer count/duration → Build → assert reel preview/download). I do this against **(a) a deterministic mock engine** (fast, hermetic CUJ replay every iteration) and **(b) a real local engine** for the true end-to-end.
- **Engine-contract live checks** — since the MVP rides existing endpoints, I exercise `/api/query → /api/compile → /api/highlights` against a running FastAPI with at least a small real/stub-indexed video, so the SPA's assumptions are tested against the real API, not a guess.
- **Automated CUJ regression** — bake the key journeys into CI (the same "catch regressions" thread) so a CUJ can't silently break.
- **Honest boundary** — the *heaviest* E2E (real 4K footage, GPU WASB, full reel encode against the golden corpus) I hand to you per our working agreement; I verify logic + UI + API-contract paths locally and say plainly what was and wasn't run on real video.

I'll make **"CUJs & live-test plan" a first-class section of this appliance design doc** (define each journey + how it's replayed live + what's automated vs owner-verified). Now let me ground the design in what the parallel sessions are actually building on the droplet/GPU side.

The hosted UI is identical across them — it enqueues a job and watches bucket/job state; only *where the worker runs* changes.
- **Cloud Run + GPU is the most interesting option economically** — scale-to-zero, pay-per-job, which lines up directly with the engine's own "per-video billing" framing in `DECOUPLED_COMPUTE` (#246). I'll flag it as the recommended direction for cost, **with an honest caveat**: it needs the worker packaged as a **container image**, and WASB's torch-1.11 conda environment (kept separate from TrackNet's TF env) makes that image non-trivial — so a quick containerization feasibility check is part of the GPU milestone, and I won't promise Cloud Run works until that's verified.
- **M1 ("GPU move ASAP")** is now provider-agnostic: *"stand up a GPU compute target + cut e2e over,"* with a recommended default and the DO/GCE/Cloud-Run options spelled out. Still parallel to the UI MVP (which runs on Gemini today).

The design workflow is still running; I'll fold the multi-provider compute axis + the containerization gap into `LOCAL_APPLIANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md` when I write it, verify the storage-layer claims (incl. the Drive stub and whether any container packaging exists) against source, and open the PR. Continuing automatically when it returns.

---
name: feedback-launch-quality-bar
description: Approaching launch — tightly isolated one-item PRs, rising test coverage, and SERIOUS observability (full log analysis across every new code path).
metadata:
  type: feedback
---

As KhelSutra inches toward launch (owner directive 2026-06-20), the quality bar for all new work is raised:

- **PRs stay tightly isolated** — strictly ONE work-item per PR, no stacking; each PR carries the tests for the code paths it adds, and **test coverage should rise, not regress**.
- **Logging & monitoring are first-class, not an afterthought** — every NEW code path gets deliberate, structured logging; do a **full log analysis across all new code paths** (correlation / job IDs threaded through the async ingest → worker → compile → download pipeline; **loud failures, never silent drops** — cf. engine #240 "surface failed chunks loudly"); add health/metrics + explicit error surfacing for the appliance, and inspect logs during CUJ live-replay.

**Why:** at launch, regressions and observability blind spots are expensive. Isolated PRs keep blast radius small and reviewable; coverage guards behavior; and serious logging/monitoring is the only way to debug a hosted multi-box system (UI + GPU worker + bucket) in production rather than guessing.

**How to apply:** every appliance / UI / per-rally PR = one item + its tests; track coverage vs the repo floor (the pre-LGTM gate) and don't let it slip. Make **Observability (logging & monitoring)** a first-class section of the appliance design doc AND a definition-of-done item, peer to the CUJ/live-test plan. Pairs with [[working-agreement-merging]] · [[working-agreement-checkpoints]] · [[launch-report-convention]] · [[feedback-tail-long-job-logs]].

373	      "live_test_plan": "First-class, layered so every CUJ is replayed LIVE throughout development — fast hermetic loop for UI, real-engine loop for contract truth, owner for hardware. Tag every assertion [verified]/[design].\n\nLAYER 1 — MOCK ENGINE (hermetic, fast, default dev + CI). Stand up a tiny FastAPI (or MSW/handler) that implements the EXACT engine contract surface the console uses — /api/upload/init|part|complete, /api/process (202+job_id), /api/jobs/{id} (scriptable state machine: queued->running with stage strings->done|failed, including the 'failed: interrupted by restart' terminal), /api/query (play_segments fixtures incl. a motion-sourced one with empty server/receiver/events to test the honesty-hiding), /api/config, /api/capabilities (one fixture per box profile: CPU-no-key, CPU-with-key, GPU), /api/compile, /api/highlights (a tiny static mp4), and the NET-NEW /api/videos + /api/reels/{video_id}. The mock lets the SPA drive ALL FOUR CUJs in <1s with no GPU/key/network. Drive it via the Claude Preview / browser tools (preview_start the Vite dev server -> preview_fill the upload picker -> preview_click through process/poll/RallyPicker -> assert the reel link via preview_snapshot/network) so each CUJ is a recorded browser replay. This is where reload-survival (CUJ-2), the query field-name bug (CUJ-3 reads play_segments), and capability honesty (CUJ-4) are caught.\n\nLAYER 2 — ENGINE-CONTRACT CHECKS against a REAL running FastAPI (semi-hermetic). Boot the actual `python -m backend.main --server` on 127.0.0.1:8000 with storage=local + detector_impl=stub + skip_ai_handoff=true (the GPU-free, key-free combo) and run contract tests that assert the mock and the real engine agree shape-for-shape: 202 payload keys, jobs status enum, query result.play_segments schema, compile {status,filepath}, highlights content-type. This is the anti-drift gate between Layer 1's mock and reality — it MUST run in CI (the engine is pip-installable, the stub needs no GPU). Any new endpoint (/api/videos, /api/reels, /api/capabilities) is born with both a mock handler AND a real-engine contract test in the same PR.\n\nLAYER 3 — REAL LOCAL E2E on the CPU droplet (the actual MVP substrate). Through the edge gate, run CUJ-1 end to end with segmenter=stub (mock detection, real plumbing) for a key-free pass, and with segmenter=gemini (real boundaries, needs key) for a real-quality pass on a SHORT clip to bound cost. Verify the reverse-proxy posture LIVE: assert /api on the public origin returns through the proxy AND that raw :8000 (and :9000/:9001/:9023) are refused from outside (firewall) — the exposure-safety invariant is a TEST, not an assumption. Run the Cloudflare-vs-droplet dual-host parity check pattern (parity.yml/parity-check.sh) for any screen that has both paths.\n\nLAYER 4 — CI REGRESSION (green-gates-merge). Reuse khelsutra test.yml (npm test) + a new job that boots the stub engine for Layer-2 contract tests; node --test / Vitest for the SPA; the browser-replay CUJ scripts run headless. One PR per progress-tracker row off origin/master, no stacking; CI green is the merge gate. Coverage stays at/above the repo floor.\n\nLAYER 5 — OWNER-ONLY HARDWARE E2E (handed off explicitly). The heaviest checks the offline suite CANNOT do: (a) native WASB byte-parity (WSL CSV == native CSV + identical candidate windows) on a REAL GPU box; (b) real-4K golden-corpus runs through gemini AND native WASB with the labelled videos; (c) Modern-Standby/keep-awake + cv2-vs-ffmpeg extraction behavior on long 4K; (d) real chunked browser upload of a multi-GB file end to end. These are owner-gated and reported back into §7; the design states plainly they were NOT verified in this offline, subprocess-mocked suite.",

510	      "design_markdown": "> **Status:** `DRAFT` — `owner-gated` · **Owner:** Avi · **Created:** 2026-06-20 · **Last updated:** 2026-06-20\n> **Lifecycle:** `DRAFT → IN PROGRESS → DONE → archived` (move to docs/archives/ when DONE)\n> **Tracking anchors:** §7 progress tracker is the source of truth; indexed in docs/README.md; pointer in NEXT_STEPS.md + memory current-state once work starts\n> **Relation to existing docs:** peer-of `PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md` / `REQUEST_ACCESS_FORM.md`; extends `UI_PROPOSAL.md` alpha screens; consumes the decoupled `StorageBackend`/`worker` seams and `HOSTING_PLAN.md` gating\n> **Honesty note:** each claim tagged [verified] / [researched] / [design]; not legal/financial advice.\n\n## 0. TL;DR\nKhelSutra Studio is a **single-box, UI-powered rally indexer + reel generator**: the khelsutra `web/` React SPA, served behind an **edge auth gate**, drives the **existing** engine FastAPI loop (upload → index → pick rallies → reel → download) on ONE machine, with `StorageBackend=local`. The single most important caveat: the engine has **zero app-layer auth and CORS `*`** [verified main.py:42], so safety is structural ONLY because the engine stays bound to `127.0.0.1:8000` [verified main.py:638], a firewall blocks inbound 8000, and a reverse proxy/tunnel is the sole authenticated ingress — **the gate, not the app, is the tenancy boundary**. On the CPU droplet real detection is **gemini (cloud)** or **stub (CPU mock)** — real local GPU detection needs the separate GPU box (native WASB, M3.5, in progress).\n\n## 1. Problem & goal\nThe decoupled engine just shipped a `StorageBackend` ABC + a `python -m backend.worker {infer|train}` dispatcher [verified backend/storage, backend/worker/__main__.py], and the FastAPI loop already covers process → jobs → query → compile → highlights [verified main.py]. What's missing is a **console** that lets a non-CLI operator run the whole loop on a single box, honestly, behind a gate. Concrete outcome: an operator signs in, uploads a clip, watches it index, picks rallies, and downloads a reel — on the live CPU droplet today (gemini/stub), and on a GPU box later (native WASB) with **no UI fork**. Read to get current: `PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md` (§3/§4/§10 contract anchors), `HOSTING_PLAN.md` (gating), `DECOUPLED_COMPUTE/HANDOFF.md` (what's built), `VIDEO_LOCALITY_MODEL.md` (privacy/retention).\n\n## 2. Decisions locked\n| # | Decision | Source / date | Implication |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| D1 | Reverse-proxied **single origin**; engine stays `127.0.0.1:8000` | [verified main.py:638]; 2026-06-20 | CORS `*` is moot from the browser; proxy is the boundary |\n| D2 | **Edge auth**, zero engine auth code | HOSTING_PLAN.md; 2026-06-20 | Caddy basicauth OR cloudflared+Cloudflare Access |\n| D3 | MVP drives the **in-process FastAPI worker** loop, `storage=local` | [verified job_worker.py, local.py:33]; 2026-06-20 | bucket worker = reserved scale path, out of MVP |\n| D4 | Embed **RallyPicker** from PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md verbatim | PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md PR #11; 2026-06-20 | console = superset, do not redesign the picker |\n| D5 | Console lives in the khelsutra **web/** React scaffold | README.md:14; 2026-06-20 | Cloudflare Pages root, not engine static |\n| D6 | Compute placement is a **setting** rendered from `/api/capabilities` | [design]; 2026-06-20 | UI offers only what the box can honestly do |\n\n## 3. Foundation — research / prior art [verified unless noted]\n- **FastAPI loop**: POST /api/process (202+job_id) → poll GET /api/jobs/{id} → POST /api/query → POST /api/compile → GET /api/highlights/{video_id}/{filename} (FileResponse) [main.py]. Chunked upload /api/upload/init|part|complete EXISTS but app.js never calls it.\n- **Worker**: one module-global `ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1)`; the DB jobs table is the clock; `fail_stale_jobs` sweeps queued/running on boot [job_worker.py].\n- **Storage**: `storage.backend='local'` is the default [models.py:298]; `fetch_to_local` is identity [local.py:33], `put` no-ops on src==dst [local.py:42] — a single box is the degenerate collapse of the control/data/compute split.\n- **Compute seam**: `detector_impl` ∈ {auto=WSL, native=NativeWasbRunner, stub=CPU mock}; base `_build_native_runner` REFUSES today [trajectory_hybrid.py:57-63]; `native_wasb_runner.py` is present on branch feat/native-wasb-runner [verified] with an owner-only byte-parity acceptance gate.\n- **Gating**: cloudflared Tunnel (outbound-only, no inbound ports) + Cloudflare Access email-OTP; backend trusts `Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email` [HOSTING_PLAN.md].\n- **Droplet reality**: CPU-only, NO GPU; co-hosts the marketing site (node :8787) + the engine (~/rally); ran the full train+infer e2e against DO Spaces with the GPU mocked [SETUP_NEW_MACHINE.md, HANDOFF.md].\n\n## 4. Design / architecture\n**Topology.** ONE box: an edge gate (cloudflared+Access or Caddy basicauth) → a reverse proxy/static host serving the `web/` SPA bundle and proxying `/api/*` to `127.0.0.1:8000` → the engine FastAPI (control plane = jobs table + max_workers=1 drain; data plane = uploads + compile + highlights) → LocalStorage → the detector seam (gemini/stub today; native WASB on a GPU box). The firewall blocks inbound 8000 + the test ports 9000/9001/9023. The marketing site stays a separate systemd unit on a separate subdomain.\n\n**Request path.** One origin to the browser; two upstreams behind it. The engine's CORS `*` is irrelevant because the proxy is the only door. Reels stream via /api/highlights; in-UI source/proxy playback has NO endpoint today (deliberate post-MVP add, tracked).\n\n**Compute placement** (one UI, surfaced from `/api/capabilities`): CPU droplet = gemini (real, cloud, key) OR stub (mock); local GPU box = native WASB (real); owner Windows = WSL `auto` (real today); remote GPU = bucket worker (reserved, out of MVP). The console renders ONLY advertised capabilities — never a `wasb_hybrid` option on a CPU box, never \"real\" when `detector_impl=stub`.\n\n**Auth/exposure.** No engine auth, by design; the gate is external. Invariant (non-negotiable): engine bound to localhost + firewall closes 8000 + edge gate up. Go-live checklist [HOSTING_PLAN.md:102-108]: Access/basicauth ON, rotate the chat-shared GEMINI_API_KEY, set `security.allowed_video_root`, rate-limit, per-user quota.\n\n**UI operator console** (web/ SPA, superset of RallyPicker): (S1) Upload & Process — wire the existing chunked endpoints, segmenter picker from /api/capabilities; (S2) Progress — 3s poll of free-text stage, \"one job at a time\", restart-failure state; (S3) Rally pick → reel — EMBED RallyPicker verbatim, honor min_shot_count, no motion server/receiver/events, no fake confidence; (S4) Reels/History — needs GET /api/videos + GET /api/reels/{video_id} for reload-survival; (S5) Settings — render config+capabilities, compute/storage target selector shown DISABLED ('single-box: local'). **Per-rally is ONE screen (S3)** of the console, not the whole thing.\n\n**REUSE MAP.** Reused (no re-plumb): the full /api loop, the in-process worker, the chunked upload endpoints (un-called), LocalStorage default, the detector seam incl. the landing NativeWasbRunner, the RallyPicker design + its anchors, the web/ scaffold, the HOSTING_PLAN gate, SETUP_NEW_MACHINE runbook. Net-new: Caddyfile + systemd units; GET /api/capabilities; GET /api/videos; GET /api/reels/{video_id}; the SPA console screens; a retention sweep; the tracked doc; (reserved) the bucket-worker bridge.\n\n## 5. Threat model / risk table\n| Threat | Today | Mitigation |\n|---|---|---|\n| No app auth, CORS `*` | publicly-reachable :8000 is wide open | localhost bind + firewall + edge gate; LIVE posture test |\n| Test ports exposed (MinIO/GCS emu) | 9000/9001/9023 on shared droplet | firewall closes them |\n| Retention | output/uploads never deleted | sweep / keep-proxy-only before non-owner exposure |\n| Surprise Gemini bill | no cost ceiling/ledger | per-video cap; ai_max_workers=1 (already) |\n| Shared-checkout collisions | active worker sessions share the tree | branch from origin/master, re-verify HEAD, explicit `git add` |\n\n## 6. Honest limits — what this does NOT do\nDoes NOT add multi-tenant identity/RLS (single-tenant alpha; uploads/outputs global). Does NOT run real WASB on the CPU droplet (gemini or stub only). Does NOT make the remote-GPU bucket path work (design only; selector disabled). Does NOT provide in-UI source playback, job cancel, or a real progress bar (coarse free-text stage, poll-only). Does NOT containerize (runbook, no Dockerfile [verified]). Does NOT verify native WASB (owner byte-parity on a real GPU box, not this offline suite).\n\n## 7. 3	Browser uploads arrive in sequential parts ≤ security.max_part_mb (free-tier edge
4	proxies cap request bodies at ~100 MB — HOSTING_PLAN §2), are assembled server-side

7	aborts partial uploads and the client re-inits. Per-user partitioning of upload_dir
8	arrives with PR-3 identity scoping.
9	
10	The startup config lives on ``backend.main.global_config`` (set ONLY in ``main()``).
11	``_upload_cfg`` resolves it through the ``backend.main`` module object so it always
12	reads the live value the server set at startup (and so tests that monkeypatch

45	    import backend.main as _main

48	    max_bytes = int(getattr(sec, "max_upload_mb", 4096) or 4096) * 1024 * 1024
49	    part_bytes = int(getattr(sec, "max_part_mb", 95) or 95) * 1024 * 1024
50	    exts = [e.lower().lstrip(".") for e in (getattr(sec, "allowed_upload_extensions", None) or ["mp4", "mov"])]
51	    return upload_dir, max_bytes, part_bytes, exts

# Single-box appliance — UI-powered rally indexer + reel generator (KhelSutra Studio)

> **Status:** `DRAFT` — owner-gated (auth choice, retention, cost ceiling, GPU host, P5 bridge) · **Owner:** Avi · **Created:** 2026-06-20 · **Last updated:** 2026-06-20
> **Lifecycle:** `DRAFT → IN PROGRESS → DONE → archived` (move to `docs/archives/` when DONE)
> **Tracking anchors:** §9 progress tracker is the source of truth; indexed in `docs/README.md`; pointer in `NEXT_STEPS.md` + memory `current-state` once work starts.
> **Relation:** peer-of `PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md` / `REQUEST_ACCESS_FORM.md` in this repo; **consumes** the engine's decoupled `StorageBackend` + `python -m backend.worker` seams and the FastAPI loop; **extends** the engine's `HOSTING_PLAN.md` gating + `UI_PROPOSAL.md` alpha screens. Engine-side changes here are **proposals to coordinate** with the active decoupled/worker sessions — not written unilaterally.
> **Honesty note:** every engine-fact claim is tagged `[verified]` (read against engine source this session), `[design]` (proposed here), or `[researched]` (needs sign-off). Not legal/financial advice.

---

## 0. TL;DR
**KhelSutra Studio** turns the engine from a CLI into a **single-box, UI-powered rally indexer + reel generator**: the khelsutra `web/` React SPA, served behind an **edge auth gate**, drives the **existing** engine FastAPI loop (ingest → index → pick rallies → reel → download) on **one machine**, with `StorageBackend=local` for the MVP. It is the **single-box deployment profile of the just-shipped decoupled architecture** — storage=local + compute=local is the degenerate collapse of the same storage/worker split.

The **single most important caveat:** the engine has **zero app-layer auth and CORS `*`** `[verified backend/main.py:42]`, so safety is structural **only** because the engine stays bound to `127.0.0.1:8000` `[verified backend/main.py:638]`, a firewall blocks inbound `8000`, and a reverse proxy/tunnel is the sole authenticated ingress — **the gate, not the app, is the tenancy boundary.**

Two honest hardware truths: (1) the current droplet is **CPU-only** → real detection there is **Gemini (cloud)** or **stub (CPU mock)**; **real local-GPU detection needs a GPU host** (native WASB, in-flight). (2) The owner priority is to **stand up a GPU host ASAP** — provider-agnostic (DO GPU droplet / GCE GPU VM / Cloud Run+GPU) — but that runs **in parallel** with the UI MVP, which ships on Gemini today.

## 1. Problem & goal
The decoupled engine just shipped a `StorageBackend` ABC + a `python -m backend.worker {infer|train}` dispatcher `[verified backend/storage/, backend/worker/__main__.py]`, and the FastAPI loop already covers `process → jobs → query → compile → highlights` `[verified backend/main.py]`. What's missing is a **console** that lets a non-CLI operator run the whole loop on a single box, **honestly, behind a gate**.

**Headline CUJ (owner's words):** a **hosted website** where I pick an input — **a Google Drive file, a bucket file (`s3://`/`gs://`), or a local upload** → it lands in the **bucket (DO Spaces / GCS)** or locally → I **generate highlights** on a **GPU-enabled host** running the real e2e → I **pick rallies, watch, and download** — **completely from the UI.**

**Outcome ("good"):** an operator signs in, picks a source, watches it index, curates rallies, downloads a reel — on the **live CPU droplet today** (Gemini/stub), and on a **GPU host later** (native WASB) with **no UI fork**.

**Read to get current:** `PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md` (§3/§4/§10 contract anchors), engine `HOSTING_PLAN.md` (gating), `DECOUPLED_COMPUTE/HANDOFF.md` (what's built), `VIDEO_LOCALITY_MODEL.md` (privacy/retention).

## 3. Foundation — what the engine gives us today  `[verified this session unless noted]`
- **FastAPI loop:** `POST /api/process` (202 + `job_id`) → poll `GET /api/jobs/{id}` → `POST /api/query` → `POST /api/compile` → `GET /api/highlights/{video_id}/{filename}` (FileResponse) `[main.py]`. **Chunked upload exists** — `POST /api/upload/init` · `PUT /api/upload/{id}/part/{i}` · `POST /api/upload/{id}/complete` · `DELETE /api/upload/{id}` `[verified uploads.py:64,89,123,147]` — but the bundled `app.js` **never calls them** (it only hits `/api/process` + `/api/compile` `[verified app.js:88,373]`). So browser upload is **client-wiring only**, no engine change.
- **In-process worker:** one module-global `ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1)` `[verified job_worker.py:37]` — strictly **one job at a time**; the DB `jobs` table is the clock; `fail_stale_jobs` sweeps queued/running on boot. Gemini chunk concurrency is a **separate** knob `indexing.ai_max_workers` (**default 5** `[verified models.py:183]`; set to 1 to serialize) — it is **not** already 1.
- **Storage:** `storage.backend` default = `"local"` `[verified models.py:298]`; `LocalStorage.fetch_to_local` is **identity** `[verified local.py:33]`, `put` no-ops on `src==dst` — so on a single box the worker's pull→pipeline→push **collapses to read-local/write-local**. URI scheme `parse_uri` `[verified ref.py]`: bare/`local://` → local; `s3://` (AWS/R2/**DO Spaces**/MinIO, by endpoint) ; `gcs://`/`gs://` ; `gdrive://` → **follow-up stub (emulated only)**. Credentials never in config — boto3 default chain / `~/.aws` `[verified s3.py]`.
- **Compute seam:** `indexing.detector_impl` ∈ {`auto`=WSL, `native`=Linux-native, `stub`=CPU mock}; default `"auto"` `[verified models.py:129]`, resolved in `trajectory_hybrid._resolve_runner` `[verified :76-85]`. **Native WASB is in-flight on branch `feat/native-wasb-runner`:** `native_wasb_runner.py` **exists** `[verified]`; **`fusion_hybrid._build_native_runner` returns `NativeWasbRunner`** `[verified fusion_hybrid.py:86]` while **`trajectory_hybrid._build_native_runner` still raises** `[verified :57-63]`. Acceptance gate = **owner byte-parity** (WSL CSV == native CSV) on a real GPU box — not exercisable on the droplet or in the offline suite. Treat native as **not production-verified**.
- **Worker CLI:** `python -m backend.worker {infer|train}` pulls a `--video-uri` → pipeline → pushes `outputs/<video_id>/{intervals.csv,report.json}` `[verified worker/__main__.py:88,149]`. The validated GPU-free/key-free combo: `detector_impl=stub` + `--set indexing.skip_ai_handoff=true` + `storage.s3`.
- **Gating:** cloudflared Tunnel (outbound-only, no inbound ports) + Cloudflare Access email-OTP; backend trusts `Cf-Access-Authenticated-User-Email` `[HOSTING_PLAN.md]`. `GET /api/health` is **expected but does not exist** `[HOSTING_PLAN.md:42]`.
- **Droplet reality:** `168.144.126.176`, **CPU-only, NO GPU**, region `blr1`; co-hosts the marketing site (`/srv/khelsutra-site`, node `:8787`) **and** the engine (`~/rally`); ran the full train+infer e2e against DO Spaces with the **GPU mocked** `[SETUP_NEW_MACHINE.md, DECOUPLED_COMPUTE/HANDOFF.md]`. Shared testbed: MinIO `9000/9001` + GCS emulator `9023` also run there.
- **Absent (net-new):** `GET /api/capabilities`, `GET /api/videos` (only single-row `get_video(id)`), `GET /api/reels/{video_id}`, any source/proxy streaming endpoint, any **Dockerfile**, any per-video **cost ledger** `[verified — grep returns none]`.

## 4. Design / architecture

### 4.1 Topology & request path
ONE box: **edge gate** (cloudflared+Access or Caddy basicauth) → **reverse proxy / static host** serving the `web/` SPA bundle and proxying `/api/*` → engine **FastAPI `127.0.0.1:8000`** → **LocalStorage** → **detector seam** (Gemini/stub today; native WASB on a GPU host). The firewall blocks inbound `8000` + the test ports `9000/9001/9023`. The marketing site stays a **separate** systemd unit on a **separate** subdomain.

```
PUBLIC INTERNET
   │  (1) EDGE GATE — the ONLY ingress; auth lives HERE, not in the app
   │      cloudflared Tunnel + Cloudflare Access (no inbound ports)  OR  Caddy :443 + basicauth
   ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ONE BOX   firewall BLOCKS inbound 8000 + 9000/9001/9023                    │
│ (2) reverse proxy / static host (Caddy/cloudflared)                        │
│      /, /assets/*  → web/ React SPA      /api/*, /api/highlights/* → :8000 │
│            │  ONE ORIGIN to the browser ⇒ engine CORS '*' is irrelevant    │
│            ▼                                                               │
│ (3) ENGINE FastAPI  (host=127.0.0.1:8000, HARD-bound [main.py:638])       │
│      control: jobs table + ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) drain         │
│      data: uploads.py chunked upload · /api/compile · /api/highlights      │
│            ▼                                                               │
│ (4) STORAGE = LocalStorage (default)  fetch=identity · put=no-op(src==dst) │
│            ▼                                                               │
│ (5) COMPUTE seam: gemini (cloud, key) · stub (CPU mock) [droplet]          │
│                   native WASB (GPU host) · auto (owner Win+WSL)            │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
RESERVED SCALE PATH (out of MVP): console "compute target" selector → storage=s3 (DO Spaces)
   → SEPARATE GPU host runs `python -m backend.worker infer --video-uri s3://…` → outputs/<vid>/ back
   (needs a NET-NEW orchestration endpoint reflecting outputs into the jobs/poll contract)
```

### 4.2 Ingest sources (the headline) — one menu, one normalized video object
All sources normalize to **"a video the pipeline can read"** — a local path (MVP) or a `StorageRef` (scale):

| Source | Path today | Status |
|---|---|---|
| **Local upload** | SPA chunks the file ≤ `max_part_mb` via the existing `/api/upload/init\|part\|complete` `[verified uploads.py]` → server path → `POST /api/process`. (Scale: also `StorageBackend.put` to `s3://`/`gcs://`.) | **wire client only** (endpoints exist) |
| **Bucket URI** (`s3://`, `gs://`) | the worker already fetches `--video-uri` → local → pipeline `[verified worker/__main__.py:98]`. From the UI, a small **ingest-to-process** step (fetch the ref → `/api/process`, or enqueue the worker) is **net-new** | **design** (storage layer ready) |
| **Google Drive** (`gdrive://`) | `parse_uri` recognizes it, but the gdrive backend is an **emulated stub** `[verified ref.py, DECOUPLED_COMPUTE research]` | **stub → roadmap;** MVP requires a Drive→bucket pre-step; do **not** present Drive as live until the backend is real |

### 4.3 Compute placement matrix (one UI, surfaced from `/api/capabilities`)
| Placement | Runs | Needs | Real/mock | Honest caveat |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **CPU droplet — gemini** (MVP target) | `segmenter=gemini`, in-proc worker | `GEMINI_API_KEY`; network; **no GPU** | **REAL** | Cloud brain: uploads ≤1280px chunks to Google — **must be in consent copy**; **no per-video cost ceiling** today |
| **CPU droplet — stub** | trajectory hybrid + `detector_impl=stub`+`skip_ai_handoff` | nothing | **MOCK** | not real tracking — UI must label "stub (CPU mock — not real detection)"; demo/regression only |
| **CPU droplet — motion** | `segmenter=motion` | nothing | REAL, low-quality | only no-key/no-GPU real segmenter; never surface `server/receiver/events` (fabricated) |
| **GPU host — native WASB** (in-flight) | `wasb`+`detector_impl=native` → `NativeWasbRunner` (in-proc torch) | a **GPU host — DO GPU droplet / GCE GPU VM / Cloud Run+GPU**; `WASB_WEIGHTS` | **REAL**, on-device | partially wired (fusion builds it, trajectory refuses); **owner byte-parity gate**; **Cloud Run/GCE need a containerized worker — no Dockerfile exists, and WASB's torch-1.11 conda env makes the image non-trivial** |
| **Owner Win+WSL — auto** | `wasb`+`detector_impl=auto` (WSL2+conda) | Windows+WSL2+GPU | REAL (today) | Windows-only transport; the working real-GPU path for owner verification |
| **Remote GPU worker via bucket** (reserved) | console CPU + `storage=s3`; separate GPU host runs `backend.worker infer` | bucket creds; a GPU host; a **net-new orchestration endpoint** | REAL (remote) | FastAPI loop and worker CLI are **separate** today; "remote GPU as a setting" is **design** — selector shown **disabled** in MVP |

**Provider note:** **Cloud Run + GPU** (scale-to-zero, pay-per-job) is the strongest fit for the engine's own **per-video-billing** economics (`DECOUPLED_COMPUTE` #246) — gated on the **containerization feasibility check** above.

### 4.4 Auth / exposure (non-negotiable invariant)
No engine auth, by design; the gate is external. **Invariant (all three must hold):** engine bound to `127.0.0.1` + firewall closes `8000` + edge gate up. Go-live checklist `[HOSTING_PLAN.md:102-108]`: Access/basicauth ON; **rotate the chat-shared `GEMINI_API_KEY`**; set `security.allowed_video_root`; rate-limit; per-user quota.

### 4.5 UI operator console (web/ SPA — superset of RallyPicker)
- **S1 Ingest & Process** — the source menu (§4.2) + a segmenter picker from `/api/capabilities` → `POST /api/process`.
- **S2 Progress** — 3s poll of the free-text stage (`hashing/validating/segmenting/finalizing`), a **"one job at a time"** notice, and the restart-failure terminal state.
- **S3 Rally pick → reel** — **embed RallyPicker verbatim** (`PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md`): honor `min_shot_count`; never render motion `server/receiver/events` or a fake confidence meter. **Per-rally is ONE screen of the console.**
- **S4 Reels / History** — needs `GET /api/videos` + `GET /api/reels/{video_id}` for **reload-survival**.
- **S5 Settings** — render `config` + `capabilities`; the compute/storage **target selector shown DISABLED** ("single-box: local") until the bucket-worker bridge lands.

### 4.6 Reuse map
**Reused (no re-plumb):** the full `/api` loop; the in-process worker; the chunked upload endpoints (un-called); LocalStorage default; the detector seam incl. the landing `NativeWasbRunner`; the **RallyPicker** design + anchors; the `web/` scaffold; the `HOSTING_PLAN` gate; the `SETUP_NEW_MACHINE` runbook. **Net-new:** Caddyfile + systemd units; `GET /api/capabilities`; `GET /api/videos`; `GET /api/reels/{video_id}`; `GET /api/health`; the SPA console screens; ingest-from-bucket wiring; a retention sweep; (reserved) the bucket-worker bridge.

## 5. Observability — logging & monitoring  `[required, first-class]`
Launch-readiness directive (owner 2026-06-20): **logs and monitoring taken seriously, with a full log-analysis pass across every new code path.** The engine already uses a centralized Python logger `[verified main.py:12-17]` — carry that discipline into every new path.

- **Correlation ID end-to-end.** Thread the existing `job_id` (from `/api/process`) **plus** an `upload_id`/`request_id` through ingest → process → jobs → compile → highlights, and into the worker for the bucket path, so **one CUJ is traceable** across UI ↔ FastAPI ↔ worker ↔ GPU host. Log it on every line.
- **Structured logs.** New endpoints/screens emit structured (JSON-able) logs at **entry / result / error** — not just "it ran". The SPA logs client-side errors + failed fetches with the same correlation id.
- **Loud failures, never silent drops.** Carry the engine's ethos (#240 "surface failed chunks loudly") + RallyPicker's `min_shot_count`-warning rule: a partial/empty reel must **never** look complete; every dropped clip/chunk is logged + surfaced in the UI.
- **Health + metrics.** Ship the missing `GET /api/health` `[HOSTING_PLAN.md:42]` and a thin metrics surface (jobs queued/running/failed, last error, disk free, per-video cost once a ledger exists) for the engine-mode banner + alerting.
- **Aggregation for transient hosts.** A scale-to-zero Cloud Run / GPU host is **ephemeral** — logs must ship to a durable sink (the bucket or a log service), not die with the instance.
- **Log-analysis pass before merge (DoD item).** Every PR that adds a code path includes a check that the path logs meaningfully, and the **logs are inspected during CUJ live-replay** (§8 Layers 1–3). Ties [[feedback-launch-quality-bar]].

## 6. Risk table
| Risk | Today | Mitigation |
|---|---|---|
| No app auth + CORS `*` `[main.py:42]` | open if any of {localhost-bind, firewall, gate} slips | enforce all three; **LIVE posture test** that raw `:8000` is refused from outside |
| Test ports on shared droplet | MinIO `9000/9001` + GCS emu `9023` | firewall closes them |
| Retention | `output/`+`uploads/` originals **never deleted** | sweep / keep-proxy-only before any non-owner exposure |
| Surprise Gemini/GPU bill | **no cost ceiling/ledger** | per-video cap; `ai_max_workers` (default 5) tunable; `max_workers=1` job serialization |
| Native WASB unverified | partially wired; owner-parity-gated | treat as not-production-verified until owner byte-parity on a real GPU box |
| New engine endpoints | `capabilities`/`videos`/`reels`/`health` don't exist | coordinate engine PRs with active sessions; **branch from `origin/master`, re-verify HEAD, explicit `git add`** |
| Drive ingest overstated | `gdrive://` is a stub | don't present Drive as live; require Drive→bucket pre-step until real |
| No Dockerfile | runbook-only reproducibility | feasibility-check a worker image before promising Cloud Run/GCE |
| Shared-checkout collisions | active worker sessions share the engine tree | the branch-safety protocol on every engine-side PR |

## 7. Honest limits — what this does NOT do
Does **not** add multi-tenant identity/RLS (single-tenant alpha; uploads/outputs global). Does **not** run real WASB on the CPU droplet (Gemini or stub only). Does **not** make the remote-GPU bucket path work (design; selector disabled). Does **not** provide in-UI source playback, job cancel, or a true progress bar (coarse free-text stage, poll-only). Does **not** containerize (runbook; no Dockerfile `[verified]`). Does **not** verify native WASB (owner byte-parity on a real GPU box, not this offline suite). A green SPA + contract tests prove the **console plumbing**, not engine rally-detection quality (a separate track).

## 8. CUJs & live-test plan  `[required]`
**CUJs:** **CUJ-1** ingest (Drive/bucket/local) → index → reel → download (core); **CUJ-2** re-open a past match after reload; **CUJ-3** pick-by-query (deterministic filters + honesty-hiding); **CUJ-4** settings/compute placement (truthful capabilities).

**Live-test plan — 5 layers, every CUJ replayed LIVE throughout development:**
- **L1 — Mock engine (hermetic, fast, default + CI).** A tiny FastAPI/MSW implementing the **exact** contract (upload; `process` 202; `jobs` state machine incl. restart-failure; `query` with a motion fixture for honesty-hiding; `config`; `capabilities` per box profile — CPU-no-key / CPU-with-key / GPU; `compile`; `highlights` tiny mp4; the new `videos`/`reels`). The SPA drives **all 4 CUJs in <1s**, **replayed via the Claude Preview/browser tools** (start dev server → fill the ingest picker → click through process/poll/RallyPicker → assert the reel link via snapshot/network). Catches reload-survival (CUJ-2), the `play_segments` field-name bug (CUJ-3), capability honesty (CUJ-4).
- **L2 — Engine-contract checks (real FastAPI, GPU-free).** Boot `python -m backend.main --server` with `storage=local` + `detector_impl=stub` + `skip_ai_handoff` and assert the **mock and the real engine agree shape-for-shape** (202 keys, jobs enum, `result.play_segments` schema, compile `{status,filepath}`, highlights content-type). Every new endpoint is born with a mock handler **and** a real-engine contract test **in the same PR**. Runs in CI.
- **L3 — Real local e2e (the MVP substrate).** Through the gate on the droplet: CUJ-1 with `stub` (key-free) and `gemini` (real, short clip to bound cost); the **exposure-safety posture test** (raw `:8000`/`:900x` refused from outside) as an assertion, not an assumption; reuse the `parity.yml` dual-host pattern.
- **L4 — CI regression (green gates merge).** `npm test` + a stub-engine contract job + headless browser-replay CUJ scripts; one small PR per tracker row off `origin/master`, **no stacking**; coverage at/above the floor; **logs inspected during replay** (§5).
- **L5 — Owner hardware e2e (handed off).** Native WASB **byte-parity** on a real GPU box; real-4K **golden-corpus** runs (Gemini + native); Modern-Standby/`cv2`-vs-`ffmpeg` behavior; a multi-GB browser upload. Reported back into §9, stated plainly as **not verified** in the offline suite.

## 10. Open questions — owner / external
**Blocking gates:** (1) **edge-auth** — Caddy basicauth (self-contained) vs cloudflared+Cloudflare Access (matches the two-host design, no inbound ports)? (2) **retention defaults** — auto-delete originals after compile? keep proxies+intervals only? window? (privacy/consent). (3) **per-video Gemini/GPU cost ceiling** — what cap, hard-fail vs warn? (4) **GPU host choice** — DO GPU droplet / GCE GPU VM / Cloud Run+GPU (and is a containerized worker worth building for Cloud Run)? (5) **P5 bridge ownership** — who builds the orchestration endpoint + how it coordinates with the active worker sessions.
**Nice-to-knows:** console home (web/ vs engine static); a review-playback streaming endpoint; per-user scoping timing; containerization vs runbook.

## 11. Definition of done
- [ ] CUJ-1 works end to end on the droplet (Gemini **and** stub) behind the gate, via the ingest menu.
- [ ] Raw `:8000`/`:900x` unreachable from outside (**tested**, not assumed).
- [ ] Local upload wired; bucket-URI ingest at least designed/tracked; Drive scoped honestly (not faked).
- [ ] RallyPicker embedded with honesty rules; `min_shot_count` surfaced.
- [ ] `/api/capabilities` + `/api/health` + banner truthful; reload-survival via `/api/videos` + `/api/reels`.
- [ ] **Observability:** correlation id threaded end-to-end; structured logs at every new path; loud failures; logs inspected during CUJ replay.
- [ ] Retention sweep in place; `GEMINI_API_KEY` rotated; `allowed_video_root` set.
- [ ] CI green (L2 contract + L4 regression); coverage ≥ floor; §9 tracker all ☑/⛔-resolved.
