### 526. assistant (2026-06-20T14:35:57.096Z)

// React state wrapper over the pure selection helpers in lib/selection.ts.
// Default = ALL selected (D8: curate-by-removal) — the user removes warm-ups.
import { useCallback, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import type { RallySegment } from "../api/types";
import * as sel from "../lib/selection";

export function useRallySelection(rallies: RallySegment[], initial: "all" | "none" = "all") {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<number>>(() =>
    initial === "none" ? sel.clear() : sel.selectAll(rallies),
  );

  const toggle = useCallback((id: number) => setSelected((s) => sel.toggle(s, id)), []);
  const selectAll = useCallback(() => setSelected(sel.selectAll(rallies)), [rallies]);
  const clearAll = useCallback(() => setSelected(sel.clear()), []);
  const invert = useCallback(() => setSelected((s) => sel.invert(s, rallies)), [rallies]);
  const isSelected = useCallback((id: number) => selected.has(id), [selected]);

  const totalSec = useMemo(() => sel.selectedDurationSec(rallies, selected), [rallies, selected]);

  return { selected, count: selected.size, totalSec, isSelected, toggle, selectAll, clearAll, invert };
}

### 528. assistant (2026-06-20T14:36:12.373Z)

// The selectable rally grid — one card per play_segment, HONEST fields only:
// ordinal (by start order), mm:ss start, duration, and a shot-count chip ONLY when present.
// Never renders server/receiver/events (motion-only fabrications, PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md §4).
import type { RallySegment } from "../api/types";
import { mmss } from "../lib/format";

interface Props {
  rallies: RallySegment[];
  isSelected: (id: number) => boolean;
  onToggle: (id: number) => void;
}

export function RallyGrid({ rallies, isSelected, onToggle }: Props) {
  if (rallies.length === 0) {
    return <p className="text-ink/60 text-sm">No rallies to show yet.</p>;
  }
  return (
    <ul className="grid grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(150px,1fr))] gap-3 list-none p-0 m-0">
      {rallies.map((r, i) => {
        const selected = isSelected(r.id);
        const shot = r.metadata?.shot_count;
        const conf = r.metadata?.shot_count_confidence;
        return (
          <li key={r.id}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onToggle(r.id)}
              aria-pressed={selected}
              aria-label={`Rally ${i + 1}${selected ? ", in reel" : ", not in reel"}`}
              className={
                "w-full text-left rounded-xl border p-3 transition " +
                (selected
                  ? "border-green bg-green/10"
                  : "border-black/10 bg-white hover:border-green/60")
              }
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="font-semibold text-ink">Rally {i + 1}</span>
                <span
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  className={
                    "inline-flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs " +
                    (selected ? "bg-green text-[#04140D]" : "border border-black/20 text-transparent")
                  }
                >
                  ✓
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="mt-1 text-sm text-ink/70">
                {mmss(r.start_time)} · {mmss(r.duration)}
              </div>
              {typeof shot === "number" && (
                <div className="mt-2 inline-block rounded-full bg-paper px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-ink/70">
                  ~{shot} shots{conf && conf !== "Unknown" ? ` · ${conf}` : ""}
                </div>
              )}
            </button>
          </li>
        );
      })}
    </ul>
  );
}

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { mmss, pluralize } from "./format";

describe("mmss", () => {
  it("formats seconds as m:ss", () => {
    expect(mmss(0)).toBe("0:00");
    expect(mmss(5)).toBe("0:05");
    expect(mmss(65)).toBe("1:05");
    expect(mmss(600)).toBe("10:00");
  });
  it("rounds to the nearest second and clamps junk to 0:00", () => {
    expect(mmss(5.7)).toBe("0:06");
    expect(mmss(-3)).toBe("0:00");
    expect(mmss(undefined as unknown as number)).toBe("0:00");
  });
});

describe("pluralize", () => {
  it("uses the singular for 1, plural otherwise", () => {
    expect(pluralize(1, "rally", "rallies")).toBe("1 rally");
    expect(pluralize(3, "rally", "rallies")).toBe("3 rallies");
    expect(pluralize(0, "rally", "rallies")).toBe("0 rallies");
    expect(pluralize(2, "clip")).toBe("2 clips");
  });
});

### 533. assistant (2026-06-20T14:36:50.064Z)

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import * as sel from "./selection";
import type { RallySegment } from "../api/types";

const mk = (id: number, dur: number): RallySegment => ({
  id, start_time: 0, end_time: dur, duration: dur, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: {},
});
const rallies = [mk(1, 5), mk(2, 10), mk(3, 7)];

describe("selection", () => {
  it("selectAll selects every id; clear empties", () => {
    expect([...sel.selectAll(rallies)].sort()).toEqual([1, 2, 3]);
    expect(sel.clear().size).toBe(0);
  });

  it("toggle adds then removes — and never mutates its input", () => {
    const s0 = sel.clear();
    const s1 = sel.toggle(s0, 2);
    expect([...s1]).toEqual([2]);
    expect(s0.size).toBe(0); // input untouched (immutable)
    expect(sel.toggle(s1, 2).size).toBe(0);
  });

  it("invert flips membership over the rally set", () => {
    expect([...sel.invert(new Set([1]), rallies)].sort()).toEqual([2, 3]);
    expect([...sel.invert(sel.selectAll(rallies), rallies)]).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("selectedDurationSec sums only the selected rows", () => {
    expect(sel.selectedDurationSec(rallies, new Set([1, 3]))).toBe(12);
    expect(sel.selectedDurationSec(rallies, sel.selectAll(rallies))).toBe(22);
    expect(sel.selectedDurationSec(rallies, sel.clear())).toBe(0);
  });

  it("selectedRallies returns the matching rows in order", () => {
    expect(sel.selectedRallies(rallies, new Set([3, 1])).map((r) => r.id)).toEqual([1, 3]);
  });
});

  { id: 6, start_time: 158, end_time: 187, duration: 29, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 24, shot_count_confidence: "High" } },
];

export default function App() {
  const sel = useRallySelection(DEMO_RALLIES, "all");

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-paper text-ink pb-24">
      <header className="bg-ink text-white">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-5xl items-center gap-1 px-6 py-4">
          <span className="text-xl font-extrabold tracking-tight">
            Khel<span className="text-green">▸</span>Sutra <span className="font-semibold text-lime">Studio</span>
          </span>
        </div>
      </header>

      <section className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-6 py-10">
        <span className="inline-flex rounded-full bg-green/15 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-green">
          per-rally selection · P2
        </span>
        <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight">Pick the rallies for your reel</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl text-ink/70">
          Tap a rally to add or remove it. Everything starts <strong>selected</strong> — just drop the ones you don't
          want. <span className="text-ink/50">(Demo data; the live list comes from the engine.)</span>
        </p>

        <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-sm">
          <button type="button" onClick={sel.selectAll} className="rounded-full border border-black/15 px-3 py-1 hover:border-green">Select all</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={sel.clearAll} className="rounded-full border border-black/15 px-3 py-1 hover:border-green">Clear</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={sel.invert} className="rounded-full border border-black/15 px-3 py-1 hover:border-green">Invert</button>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-5">
          <RallyGrid rallies={DEMO_RALLIES} isSelected={sel.isSelected} onToggle={sel.toggle} />
        </div>
      </section>

      <footer className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 border-t border-black/10 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-5xl items-center gap-4 px-6 py-3">
          <span className="text-sm text-ink/80" data-testid="selection-summary">
            <strong className="text-ink">{pluralize(sel.count, "rally", "rallies")}</strong> selected · {mmss(sel.totalSec)} total
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={sel.count === 0}
            title="Reel building wires up in P5"
            className="ml-auto rounded-full bg-green px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#04140D] disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            Build reel ({sel.count})
          </button>
        </div>
      </footer>
    </main>
  );
}

55	<body>
56	<div class="sheet">
57	  <div class="head">
58	    <span class="brand">Khel<span class="play" aria-hidden="true"></span>Sutra</span>
59	    <h1>1-page capture checklist</h1>
60	  </div>
61	  <div class="body">
62	    <p class="intro">Great highlights start with a steady, full-court recording. Five minutes of setup makes a big difference to your reels.</p>
63	
64	    <div class="rule">
65	      <span class="star" aria-hidden="true">★</span>
66	      <div>
67	        <b>The golden rule: one match, one court, in full frame — and don't move the camera.</b>
68	        <p>Multi-court footage is the hard case we're still sharpening; a single court in frame gives the best results today.</p>
69	      </div>
70	    </div>
71	
72	    <div class="grid">
73	      <div class="step">
74	        <h3><span class="n">1</span>Place the camera</h3>
75	        <ul>
76	          <li>Behind <b>one baseline</b>, elevated — a high tripod, a few metres up, or a balcony.</li>
77	          <li>Tilt down about <b>30°</b>; keep the <b>whole court</b> in frame with a little margin and net headroom.</li>
78	          <li>Players should never leave the frame.</li>
79	        </ul>
80	      </div>
81	      <div class="step">
82	        <h3><span class="n">2</span>Settings</h3>
83	        <ul>
84	          <li><b>1080p or higher</b>, shot in <b>landscape</b>.</li>
85	          <li><b>30 fps</b> for badminton/pickleball; <b>60 fps</b> for table tennis.</li>
86	          <li><b>Audio ON</b> (don't mute) — keep the mic unobstructed, wind/noise reduction off.</li>
87	        </ul>
88	      </div>
89	      <div class="step">
90	        <h3><span class="n">3</span>Keep it still</h3>
91	        <ul>
92	          <li>Fixed tripod or mount.</li>
93	          <li><b>No panning, no zooming</b> during play.</li>
94	        </ul>
95	      </div>
96	      <div class="step">
97	        <h3><span class="n">4</span>Light &amp; record</h3>
98	        <ul>
99	          <li>Even indoor lighting; avoid backlight, glare and very dark halls.</li>
100	          <li>Record the <b>whole session</b> and upload it as-is — KhelSutra does the cutting.</li>
101	        </ul>
102	      </div>
103	    </div>
104	
105	    <div class="cols">
106	      <div class="do">
107	        <h4>Do</h4>
108	        <ul>
109	          <li>Fixed camera, full court, behind the baseline</li>
110	          <li>1080p+, landscape, audio on</li>
111	          <li>One match on one court</li>
112	        </ul>
113	      </div>
114	      <div class="dont">
115	        <h4>Avoid</h4>
116	        <ul>
117	          <li>Handheld or phone-in-hand</li>
118	          <li>Broadcast-style pans &amp; zooming</li>
119	          <li>Several courts in one frame; very dark halls</li>
120	        </ul>
121	      </div>
122	    </div>
123	
124	    <p class="consent">Please record adults with their okay. Footage involving minors is never used to improve the tool and is never published.</p>
125	  </div>
126	  <div class="foot">
127	    <span>Questions? <a href="mailto:hello@khelsutra.guru">hello@khelsutra.guru</a> · <a href="https://khelsutra.guru">khelsutra.guru</a></span>
128	    <span class="noprint">Tip: press Ctrl/Cmd + P to print or save as PDF.</span>
129	  </div>
130	</div>
131	</body>
132	</html>

<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8" />
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
<title>KhelSutra — Annotate your footage & train your own model</title>
<meta name="description" content="With KhelSutra you can annotate your own footage and grow a rally model that's YOURS — on an open, MIT-licensed stack you control. The free VLC rally-annotator turns 'watch a match' into golden labels." />
<meta name="robots" content="noindex" />
<link rel="icon" href="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' viewBox='0 0 32 32'%3E%3Crect width='32' height='32' rx='8' fill='%230C261F'/%3E%3Cpath d='M13 10 L23 16 L13 22 Z' fill='%2319B36B'/%3E%3C/svg%3E" />
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" />
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin />
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Plus+Jakarta+Sans:wght@400;500;600;700;800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet" />
<style>
  :root{--ink:#0C261F;--green:#19B36B;--green-d:#137a4c;--lime:#C7F2B0;--paper:#F6F8F4;--tx:#14271F;--mut:#566B61;--line:#E1E7DF;--font:'Plus Jakarta Sans',system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif}
  *{box-sizing:border-box}
  body{margin:0;font-family:var(--font);color:var(--tx);background:#E9EDE6;line-height:1.6;-webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased}
  a{color:var(--green-d)}
  .sheet{max-width:880px;margin:18px auto;background:#fff;border-radius:16px;overflow:hidden;box-shadow:0 18px 50px -24px rgba(0,0,0,.35)}
  .head{background:var(--ink);color:#EAF3EE;padding:24px 32px}
  .head .top{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:1rem;flex-wrap:wrap}
  .brand{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.5rem;font-weight:800;font-size:1.2rem;color:#fff;text-decoration:none}
  .brand .play{display:inline-block;width:0;height:0;border-style:solid;border-width:6px 0 6px 11px;border-color:transparent transparent transparent var(--green);margin:0 .3em;transform:translateY(1px)}
  .head h1{font-size:clamp(1.5rem,3.5vw,2.1rem);font-weight:800;letter-spacing:-.02em;margin:1rem 0 0;max-width:20ch}
  .head h1 .hl{color:var(--lime)}
  .head p{color:#A7C3B7;margin:.7rem 0 0;max-width:62ch}
  .head .nav{display:flex;gap:1rem;font-size:.9rem;margin-top:1rem;flex-wrap:wrap}
  .head .nav a{color:var(--lime);text-decoration:none}
  .body{padding:26px 32px 8px}
  .sec{padding:18px 0;border-top:1px solid var(--line)}
  .sec:first-child{border-top:0}
  .kick{font-size:.78rem;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--green-d)}
  .sec h2{font-size:1.4rem;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:-.01em;margin:.35rem 0 0}
  .sec p{color:#34453d;margin:.6rem 0 0}
  .pill{display:inline-block;font-size:.72rem;font-weight:700;border-radius:999px;padding:.2rem .6rem;margin-left:.4rem;vertical-align:.12em}
  .pill.live{background:rgba(25,179,107,.14);color:#0a6c45;border:1px solid rgba(25,179,107,.35)}
  .pill.build{background:#FAEEDA;color:#854F0B;border:1px solid #EF9F27}
  ol,ul{margin:.6rem 0 0;padding-left:1.2rem}
  li{margin:.25rem 0;color:#34453d}
  li b{color:var(--tx)}
  code{font-family:ui-monospace,SFMono-Regular,Menlo,Consolas,monospace;font-size:.86em;background:#EEF3EC;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:5px;padding:.05rem .35rem}
  pre{background:#0C261F;color:#EAF3EE;border-radius:10px;padding:14px 16px;overflow:auto;margin:.8rem 0 0;font-size:.84rem;line-height:1.5}
  pre code{background:none;border:0;padding:0;color:inherit}
  .honest{display:flex;gap:.7rem;background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--line);border-left:4px solid var(--green);border-radius:0 10px 10px 0;padding:12px 16px;margin:.9rem 0 0}
  .honest b{color:var(--tx)}
  .honest p{margin:.2rem 0 0;color:var(--mut);font-size:.93rem}
  .nots{list-style:none;padding:0;margin:.8rem 0 0;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:8px}
  .nots li{display:flex;gap:.5rem;background:#FBF0ED;border:1px solid #f0d6cc;border-radius:8px;padding:.5rem .7rem;color:#6b3a2a;font-size:.9rem;margin:0}
  .moat{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px;margin-top:.8rem}
  .moat div{background:#EAF6EF;border:1px solid #BfE6D2;border-radius:10px;padding:10px 12px}
  .moat h4{margin:0;font-size:.92rem;color:var(--green-d)}
  .moat p{margin:.2rem 0 0;font-size:.86rem;color:#34453d}
  .shot{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;text-align:center;background:#EEF3EC;border:1px dashed #BfD3C6;border-radius:10px;color:var(--mut);font-size:.82rem;padding:18px;margin-top:.8rem;min-height:96px}
  .ghbtn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.5rem;background:#0C261F;color:#fff;font-weight:600;font-size:.9rem;text-decoration:none;border-radius:999px;padding:.5rem .95rem;margin-top:.9rem}
  .cta{background:#EAF6EF;border:1px solid #BfE6D2;border-radius:12px;padding:16px 18px;margin:18px 0 6px;text-align:center}
  .cta a{display:inline-block;background:var(--green);color:#04140D;font-weight:700;text-decoration:none;border-radius:999px;padding:.6rem 1.3rem;margin-top:.5rem}
  table{border-collapse:collapse;margin-top:.8rem;font-size:.86rem;width:100%}
  th,td{border:1px solid var(--line);padding:.35rem .55rem;text-align:left}
  th{background:#EEF3EC}
  .foot{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:.5rem;padding:16px 32px 26px;color:var(--mut);font-size:.85rem}
  .foot a{text-decoration:none}
  @media (max-width:680px){.nots,.moat{grid-template-columns:1fr}}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div class="sheet">
  <div class="head">
    <div class="top">
      <a class="brand" href="/">Khel<span class="play" aria-hidden="true"></span>Sutra</a>
      <a href="/#join" style="color:#C7F2B0;font-weight:600;text-decoration:none">Request access →</a>
    </div>
    <h1>Annotate your footage. <span class="hl">Train a model that's yours.</span></h1>
    <p>You can't train Gemini or GPT on your court footage. KhelSutra's direction is the inverse — a small, <b style="color:#EAF3EE">owned</b> rally model where the moat is your consented dataset, on a commercially-clean, inspectable stack you control.</p>
    <div class="nav"><a href="#annotate">Annotate</a><a href="#train">Train your own model</a><a href="#setup">Setup &amp; use</a></div>
  </div>

  <div class="body">

    <section class="sec" id="annotate">
      <div class="kick">Step 1 · real and shipping today</div>
      <h2>Annotate your footage <span class="pill live">live today</span></h2>
      <p><b>rally-annotator</b> is a free, open-source (MIT) <b>VLC plugin</b> that turns "watch a match" into golden labels. While you watch your own video in VLC, you mark each rally's start and end plus how the point ended; it writes one row per rally to a plain CSV next to your video. <b>Your footage never leaves your machine.</b> It covers five net-separated racquet sports (badminton, tennis, table tennis, pickleball, padel).</p>
      <a class="ghbtn" href="https://github.com/avidullu/rally-annotator"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8a8 8 0 0 0 5.47 7.59c.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82a7.4 7.4 0 0 1 2-.27c.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8 8 0 0 0 16 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8Z"/></svg> github.com/avidullu/rally-annotator</a>
      <div class="shot">[ screenshot: the Rally Annotator dialog open over a match in VLC — coming soon ]</div>
      <p style="margin-top:1rem"><b>The flow, per rally:</b></p>
      <ol>
        <li><b>Mark START</b> on the serve-contact frame (pause/scrub for accuracy).</li>
        <li><b>Mark END</b> when the shuttle is dead.</li>
        <li>Pick an <b>ending reason</b>, optionally a shot count, then <b>Save Rally</b> — one CSV row.</li>
        <li>Fix anything with <b>Edit / Delete / Undo</b>; re-open the same video later to continue.</li>
      </ol>
      <p style="font-size:.9rem;color:var(--mut)">It's a click-driven dialog (no marking hotkeys yet), and targets <b>VLC 3.0.x</b>.</p>
    </section>

    <section class="sec" id="train">
      <div class="kick">Step 2 · the moat</div>
      <h2>Train your own model <span class="pill build">open pipeline · in build</span></h2>
      <p>Your labels feed an <b>owned</b> rally model — trained on <b>your</b> courts, <b>your</b> angles, <b>your</b> busy multi-court halls. The pipeline is open and inspectable:</p>
      <pre><code>annotate (VLC)  →  &lt;video&gt;.rallies.csv  →  worker train  →  your model bundle (weights + a reviewable manifest)</code></pre>
      <div class="moat">
        <div><h4>You own it</h4><p>A model and dataset that are yours to keep and improve — not rented inference.</p></div>
        <div><h4>Commercially clean</h4><p>MIT/Apache/BSD-only stack; we never train on a paid LLM's output.</p></div>
        <div><h4>Private &amp; consented</h4><p>Footage stays yours; training use is explicit opt-in, never automatic.</p></div>
        <div><h4>Minors protected</h4><p>Footage involving minors is never used for training and never published.</p></div>
      </div>
      <div class="honest">
        <span aria-hidden="true" style="color:var(--green-d);font-size:1.2rem">!</span>
        <div>
          <b>Read this honestly.</b>
          <p>The labeling tool is real and shipping. The training pipeline is <b>open and in active build</b> — today it's a developer CLI; the no-GPU path trains a pipeline-validation <b>mock</b>, and a real model needs a GPU. There is <b>no "Train now" button</b> yet, and we make <b>no accuracy claim vs a paid LLM</b> (there's no such benchmark). The promise here is <b>ownership, licensing, privacy and consent</b> — not "more accurate than Gemini," and not "one click to train."</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="sec" id="setup">
      <div class="kick">Step 3 · for power users</div>
      <h2>Setup &amp; use</h2>
      <p><b>Install the plugin (VLC 3.0.x).</b> Copy <code>rally_annotator.lua</code> into VLC's per-user Lua <code>extensions</code> folder (create it if missing), then VLC → Tools → Plugins and extensions → <i>Reload extensions</i>, and enable it from <i>View → Rally Annotator</i>:</p>
      <pre><code>Windows:  %APPDATA%\vlc\lua\extensions\
macOS:    ~/Library/Application Support/org.videolan.vlc/lua/extensions/
Linux:    ~/.local/share/vlc/lua/extensions/</code></pre>
      <p style="margin-top:1rem"><b>Record</b> per the <a href="/capture">one-page capture checklist</a> (fixed tripod, full court, landscape, whole session) → <b>annotate</b> in VLC → you get <code>&lt;video&gt;.rallies.csv</code>:</p>
      <pre><code>rally_number,start_time,end_time,ending_reason,sport,shots_count
1,8.800,11.500,winner,badminton,9
2,24.389,46.589,unforced_error,badminton,21
3,49.183,54.683,let,badminton,</code></pre>
      <p style="margin-top:.6rem;font-size:.9rem;color:var(--mut)"><code>ending_reason</code> is one of: <code>unknown</code> · <code>winner</code> · <code>forced_error</code> · <code>unforced_error</code> · <code>service_fault</code> · <code>let</code> · <code>other</code>. Times are decimal seconds; downstream tools read by column name.</p>
      <p style="margin-top:1rem"><b>Train</b> (developer CLI). A GPU-free dry run validates the whole pipeline on a CPU mock; a real model needs a GPU:</p>
      <pre><code># GPU-free dry run (CPU mock — pipeline validation only):
python -m backend.worker train --corpus-uri &lt;bucket&gt; --out-uri &lt;bucket&gt; --version 0.1.0

# real trajectories (needs a GPU + the native WASB runner, in build):
python -m backend.worker train ... --trajectory-source detector --segmenter wasb_hybrid</code></pre>
      <p style="margin-top:.6rem;font-size:.9rem;color:var(--mut)">One join rule: keep the video <b>byte-identical</b> to what you annotated (don't re-encode after labeling) — labels match videos by content hash.</p>
    </section>

    <section class="sec">
      <div class="kick">So expectations stay honest</div>
      <h2>What this is not</h2>
      <ul class="nots">
        <li>Not a one-click trainer — training is an offline CLI today.</li>
        <li>Not in-browser — labeling runs in VLC on your machine.</li>
        <li>Not an accuracy claim vs Gemini/GPT — no such benchmark.</li>
        <li>Shot-type, player and score detection are roadmap.</li>
      </ul>
    </section>

    <div class="cta">
      <b>Want in?</b>
      <div><a href="/#join">Request alpha access</a></div>
    </div>

  </div>
  <div class="foot">
    <span><a href="/">khelsutra.guru</a> · <a href="https://github.com/avidullu/rally-annotator">rally-annotator (MIT)</a></span>
    <span>Reels carry a "Generated by Khelsutra.guru" mark.</span>
  </div>
</div>
</body>
</html>

### 633. user (2026-06-20T14:55:46.706Z)

we now have a video on the website which we show a clip of when someone signs up, can we not reuse that for the screenshots?

### 634. assistant (2026-06-20T14:57:41.030Z)

Good question — and it's worth being precise, because the video and the placeholder are actually two different things:

**The video is a highlight *reel* (the engine's output — badminton play), not a screenshot of the *rally-annotator tool*.** The own-model page's placeholder is for "the Rally Annotator dialog open over a match in VLC" — i.e., the labeling UI (the Mark START/END buttons, the dialog, the CSV). The reel doesn't show any of that, so it can't stand in for the tool screenshot. A genuine "here's how you annotate" visual still needs a capture of VLC + the plugin (which only you can grab on your machine).

**But yes — we can reuse a real still from the video as an honest illustrative visual.** A frame of the actual session (e.g. the YouTube thumbnail) legitimately shows "the kind of footage you record and annotate / the reel KhelSutra makes," and it's already consented content. 40	  code{font-family:ui-monospace,SFMono-Regular,Menlo,Consolas,monospace;font-size:.86em;background:#EEF3EC;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:5px;padding:.05rem .35rem}
41	  pre{background:#0C261F;color:#EAF3EE;border-radius:10px;padding:14px 16px;overflow:auto;margin:.8rem 0 0;font-size:.84rem;line-height:1.5}
42	  pre code{background:none;border:0;padding:0;color:inherit}
43	  .honest{display:flex;gap:.7rem;background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--line);border-left:4px solid var(--green);border-radius:0 10px 10px 0;padding:12px 16px;margin:.9rem 0 0}
44	  .honest b{color:var(--tx)}
45	  .honest p{margin:.2rem 0 0;color:var(--mut);font-size:.93rem}
46	  .nots{list-style:none;padding:0;margin:.8rem 0 0;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:8px}
47	  .nots li{display:flex;gap:.5rem;background:#FBF0ED;border:1px solid #f0d6cc;border-radius:8px;padding:.5rem .7rem;color:#6b3a2a;font-size:.9rem;margin:0}
48	  .moat{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px;margin-top:.8rem}
49	  .moat div{background:#EAF6EF;border:1px solid #BfE6D2;border-radius:10px;padding:10px 12px}
50	  .moat h4{margin:0;font-size:.92rem;color:var(--green-d)}

52	  .shot{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;text-align:center;background:#EEF3EC;border:1px dashed #BfD3C6;border-radius:10px;color:var(--mut);font-size:.82rem;padding:18px;margin-top:.8rem;min-height:96px}
53	  .ghbtn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.5rem;background:#0C261F;color:#fff;font-weight:600;font-size:.9rem;text-decoration:none;border-radius:999px;padding:.5rem .95rem;margin-top:.9rem}
54	  .cta{background:#EAF6EF;border:1px solid #BfE6D2;border-radius:12px;padding:16px 18px;margin:18px 0 6px;text-align:center}
55	  .cta a{display:inline-block;background:var(--green);color:#04140D;font-weight:700;text-decoration:none;border-radius:999px;padding:.6rem 1.3rem;margin-top:.5rem}
56	  table{border-collapse:collapse;margin-top:.8rem;font-size:.86rem;width:100%}
57	  th,td{border:1px solid var(--line);padding:.35rem .55rem;text-align:left}
58	  th{background:#EEF3EC}
59	  .foot{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:.5rem;padding:16px 32px 26px;color:var(--mut);font-size:.85rem}
60	  .foot a{text-decoration:none}
61	  @media (max-width:680px){.nots,.moat{grid-template-columns:1fr}}
62	  /* sample reel — click-to-play; a BRANDED poster (no footage/faces) until the user taps */
63	  .reelwrap{position:relative;max-width:680px;margin:0 auto;aspect-ratio:16/9;border-radius:14px;overflow:hidden;border:1px solid var(--line);background:#0C261F}
64	  .reelwrap iframe,.reelwrap>div{width:100%;height:100%;border:0;display:block}
65	  .reelposter{position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;border:0;cursor:pointer;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:.7rem;background:linear-gradient(160deg,#0C261F,#123A2E);color:#EAF3EE}
66	  .reelposter .pbtn{width:64px;height:64px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--lime);color:#0C261F;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:1.5rem;padding-left:4px}
67	  .reelposter .pcap{font-size:.92rem;color:#A7C3B7}
68	</style>
69	</head>
70	<body>
71	<div class="sheet">
72	  <div class="head">
73	    <div class="top">
74	      <a class="brand" href="/">Khel<span class="play" aria-hidden="true"></span>Sutra</a>
75	      <a href="/#join" style="color:#C7F2B0;font-weight:600;text-decoration:none">Request access →</a>
76	    </div>
77	    <h1>Annotate your footage. <span class="hl">Train a model that's yours.</span></h1>
78	    <p>You can't train Gemini or GPT on your court footage. KhelSutra's direction is the inverse — a small, <b style="color:#EAF3EE">owned</b> rally model where the moat is your consented dataset, on a commercially-clean, inspectable stack you control.</p>
79	    <div class="nav"><a href="#annotate">Annotate</a><a href="#train">Train your own model</a><a href="#setup">Setup &amp; use</a></div>
80	  </div>
81	
82	  <div class="body">
83	
84	    <div style="margin:4px 0 10px">
85	      <div class="reelwrap">
86	        <button type="button" class="reelposter" id="om-reel-play" aria-label="Play a sample reel made by KhelSutra">
87	          <span class="pbtn" aria-hidden="true">▶</span>
88	          <span class="pcap">A real reel, made by KhelSutra — tap to play</span>
89	        </button>
90	      </div>
91	      <p style="text-align:center;color:var(--mut);font-size:.82rem;margin:.5rem 0 0">A real recorded session, cut automatically — plays from a random point; the footage stays hidden until you tap.</p>
92	    </div>
93	
94	    <section class="sec" id="annotate">
95	      <div class="kick">Step 1 · real and shipping today</div>
96	      <h2>Annotate your footage <span class="pill live">live today</span></h2>
97	      <p><b>rally-annotator</b> is a free, open-source (MIT) <b>VLC plugin</b> that turns "watch a match" into golden labels. While you watch your own video in VLC, you mark each rally's start and end plus how the point ended; it writes one row per rally to a plain CSV next to your video. <b>Your footage never leaves your machine.</b> It covers five net-separated racquet sports (badminton, tennis, table tennis, pickleball, padel).</p>
98	      <a class="ghbtn" href="https://github.com/Khelsutra/rally-annotator"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 0C3.58 0 0 3.58 0 8a8 8 0 0 0 5.47 7.59c.4.07.55-.17.55-.38 0-.19-.01-.82-.01-1.49-2.01.37-2.53-.49-2.69-.94-.09-.23-.48-.94-.82-1.13-.28-.15-.68-.52-.01-.53.63-.01 1.08.58 1.23.82.72 1.21 1.87.87 2.33.66.07-.52.28-.87.51-1.07-1.78-.2-3.64-.89-3.64-3.95 0-.87.31-1.59.82-2.15-.08-.2-.36-1.02.08-2.12 0 0 .67-.21 2.2.82a7.4 7.4 0 0 1 2-.27c.68 0 1.36.09 2 .27 1.53-1.04 2.2-.82 2.2-.82.44 1.1.16 1.92.08 2.12.51.56.82 1.27.82 2.15 0 3.07-1.87 3.75-3.65 3.95.29.25.54.73.54 1.48 0 1.07-.01 1.93-.01 2.2 0 .21.15.46.55.38A8 8 0 0 0 16 8c0-4.42-3.58-8-8-8Z"/></svg> github.com/Khelsutra/rally-annotator</a>
99	      <p style="margin-top:1rem"><b>The flow, per rally:</b></p>
100	      <ol>
101	        <li><b>Mark START</b> on the serve-contact frame (pause/scrub for accuracy).</li>
102	        <li><b>Mark END</b> when the shuttle is dead.</li>
103	        <li>Pick an <b>ending reason</b>, optionally a shot count, then <b>Save Rally</b> — one CSV row.</li>
104	        <li>Fix anything with <b>Edit / Delete / Undo</b>; re-open the same video later to continue.</li>
105	      </ol>
106	      <p style="font-size:.9rem;color:var(--mut)">It's a click-driven dialog (no marking hotkeys yet), and targets <b>VLC 3.0.x</b>.</p>
107	    </section>
108	
109	    <section class="sec" id="train">
110	      <div class="kick">Step 2 · the moat</div>
111	      <h2>Train your own model <span class="pill build">open pipeline · in build</span></h2>
112	      <p>Your labels feed an <b>owned</b> rally model — trained on <b>your</b> courts, <b>your</b> angles, <b>your</b> busy multi-court halls. The pipeline is open and inspectable:</p>
113	      <pre><code>annotate (VLC)  →  &lt;video&gt;.rallies.csv  →  worker train  →  your model bundle (weights + a reviewable manifest)</code></pre>
114	      <div class="moat">
115	        <div><h4>You own it</h4><p>A model and dataset that are yours to keep and improve — not rented inference.</p></div>
116	        <div><h4>Commercially clean</h4><p>MIT/Apache/BSD-only stack; we never train on a paid LLM's output.</p></div>
117	        <div><h4>Private &amp; consented</h4><p>Footage stays yours; training use is explicit opt-in, never automatic.</p></div>
118	        <div><h4>Minors protected</h4><p>Footage involving minors is never used for training and never published.</p></div>
119	      </div>
120	      <div class="honest">
121	        <span aria-hidden="true" style="color:var(--green-d);font-size:1.2rem">!</span>
122	        <div>
123	          <b>Read this honestly.</b>
124	          <p>The labeling tool is real and shipping. The training pipeline is <b>open and in active build</b> — today it's a developer CLI; the no-GPU path trains a pipeline-validation <b>mock</b>, and a real model needs a GPU. There is <b>no "Train now" button</b> yet, and we make <b>no accuracy claim vs a paid LLM</b> (there's no such benchmark). The promise here is <b>ownership, licensing, privacy and consent</b> — not "more accurate than Gemini," and not "one click to train."</p>
125	        </div>
126	      </div>
127	    </section>
128	
129	    <section class="sec" id="setup">
130	      <div class="kick">Step 3 · for power users</div>
131	      <h2>Setup &amp; use</h2>
132	      <p><b>Install the plugin (VLC 3.0.x).</b> Copy <code>rally_annotator.lua</code> into VLC's per-user Lua <code>extensions</code> folder (create it if missing), then VLC → Tools → Plugins and extensions → <i>Reload extensions</i>, and enable it from <i>View → Rally Annotator</i>:</p>
133	      <pre><code>Windows:  %APPDATA%\vlc\lua\extensions\
134	macOS:    ~/Library/Application Support/org.videolan.vlc/lua/extensions/
135	Linux:    ~/.local/share/vlc/lua/extensions/</code></pre>
136	      <p style="margin-top:1rem"><b>Record</b> per the <a href="/capture">one-page capture checklist</a> (fixed tripod, full court, landscape, whole session) → <b>annotate</b> in VLC → you get <code>&lt;video&gt;.rallies.csv</code>:</p>
137	      <pre><code>rally_number,start_time,end_time,ending_reason,sport,shots_count
138	1,8.800,11.500,winner,badminton,9
139	2,24.389,46.589,unforced_error,badminton,21
140	3,49.183,54.683,let,badminton,</code></pre>
141	      <p style="margin-top:.6rem;font-size:.9rem;color:var(--mut)"><code>ending_reason</code> is one of: <code>unknown</code> · <code>winner</code> · <code>forced_error</code> · <code>unforced_error</code> · <code>service_fault</code> · <code>let</code> · <code>other</code>. Times are decimal seconds; downstream tools read by column name.</p>
142	      <p style="margin-top:1rem"><b>Train</b> (developer CLI). A GPU-free dry run validates the whole pipeline on a CPU mock; a real model needs a GPU:</p>
143	      <pre><code># GPU-free dry run (CPU mock — pipeline validation only):
144	python -m backend.worker train --corpus-uri &lt;bucket&gt; --out-uri &lt;bucket&gt; --version 0.1.0
145	
146	# real trajectories (needs a GPU + the native WASB runner, in build):
147	python -m backend.worker train ... --trajectory-source detector --segmenter wasb_hybrid</code></pre>
148	      <p style="margin-top:.6rem;font-size:.9rem;color:var(--mut)">One join rule: keep the video <b>byte-identical</b> to what you annotated (don't re-encode after labeling) — labels match videos by content hash.</p>
149	    </section>
150	
151	    <section class="sec">
152	      <div class="kick">So expectations stay honest</div>
153	      <h2>What this is not</h2>
154	      <ul class="nots">
155	        <li>Not a one-click trainer — training is an offline CLI today.</li>
156	        <li>Not in-browser — labeling runs in VLC on your machine.</li>
157	        <li>Not an accuracy claim vs Gemini/GPT — no such benchmark.</li>
158	        <li>Shot-type, player and score detection are roadmap.</li>
159	      </ul>
160	    </section>
161	
162	    <div class="cta">
163	      <b>Want in?</b>
164	      <div><a href="/#join">Request alpha access</a></div>
165	    </div>
166	
167	  </div>
168	  <div class="foot">
169	    <span><a href="/">khelsutra.guru</a> · <a href="https://github.com/Khelsutra/rally-annotator">rally-annotator (MIT)</a></span>
170	    <span>Reels carry a "Generated by Khelsutra.guru" mark.</span>
171	  </div>
172	</div>
173	<script>
174	(function(){
175	  var b = document.getElementById('om-reel-play');
176	  if(!b) return;
177	  var VIDEO = 'ajTCZ7pJW_o', MIN = 90;
178	  function loadYT(cb){
179	    if(window.YT && window.YT.Player) return cb();
180	    var prev = window.onYouTubeIframeAPIReady;
181	    window.onYouTubeIframeAPIReady = function(){ if(typeof prev === 'function'){ prev(); } cb(); };
182	    if(!document.getElementById('yt-iframe-api')){
183	      var s = document.createElement('script');
184	      s.id = 'yt-iframe-api'; s.src = 'https://www.youtube.com/iframe_api';
185	      document.head.appendChild(s);
186	    }
187	  }
188	  b.addEventListener('click', function(){
189	    var wrap = b.parentNode, slot = document.createElement('div');
190	    slot.id = 'om-reel-player';
191	    wrap.replaceChild(slot, b);
192	    loadYT(function(){
193	      new YT.Player('om-reel-player', {
194	        width: '100%', height: '100%', videoId: VIDEO,
195	        host: 'https://www.youtube-nocookie.com',
196	        playerVars: { autoplay: 1, rel: 0, modestbranding: 1, playsinline: 1 },
197	        events: { onReady: function(e){
198	          var dur = Math.floor(e.target.getDuration() || 0);
199	          var maxStart = Math.max(0, dur - MIN);        // start at least 90s before the end
200	          var start = maxStart > 0 ? Math.floor(Math.random() * (maxStart + 1)) : 0;
201	          e.target.seekTo(start, true);
202	          e.target.playVideo();
203	        } }
204	      });
205	    });
206	  });
207	})();
208	</script>
209	</body>
210	</html>

104	blocker) produces trajectories; the owned IP = featurizer + head + corpus + harness/gate. **Gen-2 (Qwen3-VL via
105	ms-swift) = pixels, drops WASB** — that's the heavyweight step, only entered if the lightweight head plateaus below
106	target. Gen-0 trains in MINUTES on the 2070S ($0). Multisport: WASB transfers to TT (0.909) but not pickleball
107	(0.308) → per-sport detectors or Gen-2.

154	- PowerShell `ConvertTo-Json` reformats config.json wholesale — edit surgically. `Select-Object -Last N` buffers
155	  background output until exit (no interim tail) — and loses EVERYTHING if the process is killed (idle/suspend kills
156	  background runs silently); pipe long background runs through `Out-File` instead.
